===== resources/js/pages/Calendar/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
   3 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   4 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   5 | import type { CalendarEventRow } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   6 | import { EVENT_TYPE_COLORS } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   7 | import { CalendarSidebar } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarSidebar';
   8 | import { CalendarToolbar } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarToolbar';
   9 | import { CalendarEventPill } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarEventPill';
  10 | import { CalendarEventDrawer } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarEventDrawer';
  11 | import { CalendarRightPanel } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarRightPanel';
  12 | import FullCalendar from '@fullcalendar/react';
  13 | import dayGridPlugin from '@fullcalendar/daygrid';
  14 | import timeGridPlugin from '@fullcalendar/timegrid';
  15 | import interactionPlugin from '@fullcalendar/interaction';
  16 | import type { EventClickArg, DateSelectArg, EventDropArg, EventResizeDoneArg } from '@fullcalendar/core';
  17 | 
  18 | type PageProps = {
  19 |     events: CalendarEventRow[];
  20 |     users: { id: number; name: string; email: string }[];
  21 | };
  22 | 
  23 | function toFC(event: CalendarEventRow) {
  24 |     const color = event.color || EVENT_TYPE_COLORS[event.type] || '#6b7280';
  25 |     return {
  26 |         id: String(event.id),
  27 |         title: event.title,
  28 |         start: event.startsAt,
  29 |         end: event.endsAt || undefined,
  30 |         allDay: event.allDay,
  31 |         backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  32 |         borderColor: 'transparent',
  33 |         textColor: color,
  34 |         extendedProps: {
  35 |             type: event.type,
  36 |             description: event.description,
  37 |             status: event.status,
  38 |             priority: event.priority,
  39 |             eventNumber: event.eventNumber,
  40 |         },
  41 |     };
  42 | }
  43 | 
  44 | export default function CalendarIndex({ events: _events, users }: PageProps) {
  45 |     const initialEvents = Array.isArray(_events) ? _events : [];
  46 |     const calendarRef = useRef<FullCalendar>(null);
  47 |     const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(new Date());
  48 |     const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'dayGridMonth' | 'timeGridWeek' | 'timeGridDay'>('dayGridMonth');
  49 |     const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  50 |     const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('');
  51 |     const [filterUserId, setFilterUserId] = useState('');
  52 |     const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  53 |     const [editEvent, setEditEvent] = useState<CalendarEventRow | undefined>(undefined);
  54 |     const [defaultStart, setDefaultStart] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  55 | 
  56 |     const fcEvents = useMemo(() => initialEvents.filter((e) => {
  57 |         if (search.trim()) {
  58 |             const q = search.toLowerCase();
  59 |             if (!e.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !(e.description || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
  60 |         }
  61 |         if (filterType && e.type !== filterType) return false;
  62 |         if (filterUserId) {
  63 |             const uid = Number(filterUserId);
  64 |             const parts = e.participants || [];
  65 |             if (!parts.some((p) => p.userId === uid) && e.createdBy?.id !== uid) return false;
  66 |         }
  67 |         return true;
  68 |     }).map(toFC), [initialEvents, search, filterType, filterUserId]);
  69 | 
  70 |     function handleViewChange(v: typeof viewMode) {
  71 |         setViewMode(v);
  72 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  73 |         if (api) api.changeView(v);
  74 |     }
  75 | 
  76 |     function handlePrev() {
  77 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  78 |         if (api) api.prev();
  79 |     }
  80 | 
  81 |     function handleNext() {
  82 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  83 |         if (api) api.next();
  84 |     }
  85 | 
  86 |     function handleToday() {
  87 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  88 |         if (api) api.today();
  89 |     }
  90 | 
  91 |     const handleDatesSet = useCallback((arg: { start: Date; end: Date; view: { currentStart: Date } }) => {
  92 |         setCurrentDate(arg.view.currentStart);
  93 |     }, []);
  94 | 
  95 |     function handleEventClick(arg: EventClickArg) {
  96 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
  97 |         const found = initialEvents.find((e) => e.id === id);
  98 |         if (found) {
  99 |             fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}`)
 100 |                 .then((r) => r.json())
 101 |                 .then((data) => {
 102 |                     setEditEvent(data.data || found);
 103 |                     setDefaultStart(undefined);
 104 |                     setDrawerOpen(true);
 105 |                 })
 106 |                 .catch(() => {
 107 |                     setEditEvent(found);
 108 |                     setDefaultStart(undefined);
 109 |                     setDrawerOpen(true);
 110 |                 });
 111 |         }
 112 |     }
 113 | 
 114 |     function handleSidebarEventClick(event: CalendarEventRow) {
 115 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${event.id}`)
 116 |             .then((r) => r.json())
 117 |             .then((data) => {
 118 |                 setEditEvent(data.data || event);
 119 |                 setDefaultStart(undefined);
 120 |                 setDrawerOpen(true);
 121 |             })
 122 |             .catch(() => {
 123 |                 setEditEvent(event);
 124 |                 setDefaultStart(undefined);
 125 |                 setDrawerOpen(true);
 126 |             });
 127 |     }
 128 | 
 129 |     function handleDateSelect(arg: DateSelectArg) {
 130 |         setEditEvent(undefined);
 131 |         setDefaultStart(arg.startStr);
 132 |         setDrawerOpen(true);
 133 |     }
 134 | 
 135 |     function openCreateDrawer() {
 136 |         setEditEvent(undefined);
 137 |         setDefaultStart(undefined);
 138 |         setDrawerOpen(true);
 139 |     }
 140 | 
 141 |     function handleEventDrop(arg: EventDropArg) {
 142 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
 143 |         const startStr = arg.event.start?.toISOString();
 144 |         const endStr = arg.event.end?.toISOString();
 145 | 
 146 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}/move`, {
 147 |             method: 'PUT',
 148 |             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': (window as any).csrfToken || '' },
 149 |             body: JSON.stringify({ starts_at: startStr, ends_at: endStr }),
 150 |         })
 151 |             .then((r) => {
 152 |                 if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Move failed');
 153 |                 toast.success('Event moved.');
 154 |             })
 155 |             .catch(() => {
 156 |                 arg.revert();
 157 |                 toast.error('Failed to move event.');
 158 |             });
 159 |     }
 160 | 
 161 |     function handleEventResize(arg: EventResizeDoneArg) {
 162 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
 163 |         const endStr = arg.event.end?.toISOString();
 164 | 
 165 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}/resize`, {
 166 |             method: 'PUT',
 167 |             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': (window as any).csrfToken || '' },
 168 |             body: JSON.stringify({ ends_at: endStr }),
 169 |         })
 170 |             .then((r) => {
 171 |                 if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Resize failed');
 172 |                 toast.success('Event resized.');
 173 |             })
 174 |             .catch(() => {
 175 |                 arg.revert();
 176 |                 toast.error('Failed to resize event.');
 177 |             });
 178 |     }
 179 | 
 180 |     const headerAction = (
 181 |         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 182 |             <button type="button" onClick={handleToday} className="h-8 rounded-lg border border-white/8 px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white/70 transition hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white">Today</button>
 183 |             <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-white/8 p-0.5">
 184 |                 {(['dayGridMonth', 'timeGridWeek', 'timeGridDay'] as const).map((v) => (
 185 |                     <button key={v} type="button" onClick={() => handleViewChange(v)}
 186 |                         className={`h-7 rounded-md px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold transition ${viewMode === v ? 'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black' : 'text-white/50 hover:text-white'}`}>
 187 |                         {v === 'dayGridMonth' ? 'Month' : v === 'timeGridWeek' ? 'Week' : 'Day'}
 188 |                     </button>
 189 |                 ))}
 190 |             </div>
 191 |             <button type="button" onClick={openCreateDrawer} className="flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[var(--crm-gold)] px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-black transition hover:brightness-110">
 192 |                 <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>
 193 |                 New event
 194 |             </button>
 195 |         </div>
 196 |     );
 197 | 
 198 |     return (
 199 |         <>
 200 |             <Head title="Calendar" />
 201 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.calendar" titleKey="nav.calendar" subtitleKey="Schedule tasks, notes, reminders, and follow-ups." action={headerAction}>
 202 |                 <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-4">
 203 |                     <div className="grid grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)_320px] gap-4 max-xl:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)] max-md:grid-cols-1">
 204 |                         <CalendarSidebar
 205 |                             search={search}
 206 |                             onSearchChange={setSearch}
 207 |                             filterType={filterType}
 208 |                             onFilterTypeChange={setFilterType}
 209 |                             filterUserId={filterUserId}
 210 |                             onFilterUserIdChange={setFilterUserId}
 211 |                             currentDate={currentDate}
 212 |                             onDateChange={(d) => { setCurrentDate(d); calendarRef.current?.getApi()?.gotoDate(d); }}
 213 |                             onDayClick={(d) => { calendarRef.current?.getApi()?.gotoDate(d); setViewMode('timeGridDay'); }}
 214 |                             users={users}
 215 |                             onCreateClick={openCreateDrawer}
 216 |                             events={initialEvents}
 217 |                             onEventClick={handleSidebarEventClick}
 218 |                         />
 219 | 
 220 |                         <div className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707]">
 221 |                             <CalendarToolbar
 222 |                                 currentDate={currentDate}
 223 |                                 viewMode={viewMode}
 224 |                                 onViewModeChange={handleViewChange}
 225 |                                 onPrev={handlePrev}
 226 |                                 onNext={handleNext}
 227 |                                 onToday={handleToday}
 228 |                             />
 229 |                             <div className="p-0">
 230 |                                 <FullCalendar
 231 |                                     ref={calendarRef}
 232 |                                     plugins={[dayGridPlugin, timeGridPlugin, interactionPlugin]}
 233 |                                     initialView={viewMode}
 234 |                                     events={fcEvents}
 235 |                                     eventContent={CalendarEventPill}
 236 |                                     eventClick={handleEventClick}
 237 |                                     selectable={true}
 238 |                                     select={handleDateSelect}
 239 |                                     datesSet={handleDatesSet}
 240 |                                     dayMaxEvents={3}
 241 |                                     height="auto"
 242 |                                     headerToolbar={false}
 243 |                                     firstDay={1}
 244 |                                     slotMinTime="07:00:00"
 245 |                                     slotMaxTime="20:00:00"
 246 |                                     allDaySlot={true}
 247 |                                     nowIndicator={true}
 248 |                                     editable={true}
 249 |                                     eventDrop={handleEventDrop}
 250 |                                     eventResize={handleEventResize}
 251 |                                     eventDurationEditable={true}
 252 |                                     eventStartEditable={true}
 253 |                                 />
 254 |                             </div>
 255 |                         </div>
 256 | 
 257 |                         <div className="max-xl:hidden">
 258 |                             <CalendarRightPanel
 259 |                                 events={initialEvents}
 260 |                                 onEventClick={handleSidebarEventClick}
 261 |                                 onCreateEvent={openCreateDrawer}
 262 |                             />
 263 |                         </div>
 264 |                     </div>
 265 |                 </div>
 266 |             </AppShell>
 267 | 
 268 |             <CalendarEventDrawer
 269 |                 isOpen={drawerOpen}
 270 |                 onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) { setDrawerOpen(false); setEditEvent(undefined); } }}
 271 |                 users={users}
 272 |                 editEvent={editEvent}
 273 |                 defaultStart={defaultStart}
 274 |             />
 275 |         </>
 276 |     );
 277 | }

===== resources/js/pages/Tasks/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2, Clock3, Flame, Plus, ShieldAlert } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { FormEvent, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   6 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   7 | import type { FormErrors } from '@/lib/formErrors';
   8 | import type { TaskRow, TaskStatus, UserOption } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   9 | import { COLUMNS } from '@/features/tasks/types';
  10 | import { TaskFilters } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskFilters';
  11 | import { TaskBoard } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskBoard';
  12 | import { TaskCalendar } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskCalendar';
  13 | import { TaskList } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskList';
  14 | import { TaskCreateDrawer } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskCreateDrawer';
  15 | import { TaskDetailDrawer } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskDetailDrawer';
  16 | import { TaskRequestCreateDrawer, type TaskRequestOptions } from '@/features/tasks/components/TaskRequestCreateDrawer';
  17 | 
  18 | type PageProps = {
  19 |     tasks: TaskRow[];
  20 |     users: UserOption[];
  21 |     activeFilter: string;
  22 |     activeCategory: string;
  23 |     taskRequestTypes: string[];
  24 |     taskRequestTypeLabels: Record<string, string>;
  25 |     taskRequestOptions: TaskRequestOptions;
  26 | };
  27 | 
  28 | function isOpenTask(task: TaskRow) {
  29 |     return task.status !== 'completed' && task.status !== 'cancelled';
  30 | }
  31 | 
  32 | function isOverdue(task: TaskRow) {
  33 |     return Boolean(task.dueDate && new Date(task.dueDate) < new Date() && isOpenTask(task));
  34 | }
  35 | 
  36 | function OperationsMetric({
  37 |     label,
  38 |     value,
  39 |     helper,
  40 |     tone,
  41 |     icon: Icon,
  42 | }: {
  43 |     label: string;
  44 |     value: string | number;
  45 |     helper: string;
  46 |     tone: 'gold' | 'red' | 'green' | 'blue';
  47 |     icon: typeof Flame;
  48 | }) {
  49 |     const colors = {
  50 |         gold: 'text-[var(--crm-gold)] bg-[var(--crm-gold-soft)]',
  51 |         red: 'text-red-300 bg-red-500/10',
  52 |         green: 'text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-500/10',
  53 |         blue: 'text-blue-300 bg-blue-500/10',
  54 |     };
  55 | 
  56 |     return (
  57 |         <div className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[linear-gradient(135deg,var(--crm-elevated),rgba(0,0,0,0.18))] p-3">
  58 |             <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
  59 |                 <div>
  60 |                     <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{label}</p>
  61 |                     <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black leading-none text-[var(--crm-text)]">{value}</p>
  62 |                     <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{helper}</p>
  63 |                 </div>
  64 |                 <span className={`flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${colors[tone]}`}>
  65 |                     <Icon size={16} />
  66 |                 </span>
  67 |             </div>
  68 |         </div>
  69 |     );
  70 | }
  71 | 
  72 | export default function TasksIndex({ tasks, users, activeFilter, activeCategory, taskRequestTypes, taskRequestTypeLabels, taskRequestOptions }: PageProps) {
  73 |     const [localTasks, setLocalTasks] = useState<TaskRow[]>(tasks);
  74 |     const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  75 |     const [filter, setFilter] = useState(activeFilter);
  76 |     const [category, setCategory] = useState(activeCategory);
  77 |     const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'board' | 'list' | 'calendar'>('board');
  78 |     const [selectedTask, setSelectedTask] = useState<TaskRow | null>(null);
  79 |     const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  80 |     const [requestOpen, setRequestOpen] = useState(false);
  81 |     const [form, setForm] = useState({
  82 |         title: '', description: '', status: 'not_started' as string,
  83 |         priority: 'medium' as string, category: 'general_admin' as string,
  84 |         due_date: '', assignee_ids: [] as number[], watcher_ids: [] as number[],
  85 |     });
  86 |     const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  87 | 
  88 |     const filtered = useMemo(() => {
  89 |         let items = localTasks;
  90 |         if (query.trim()) {
  91 |             const q = query.toLowerCase();
  92 |             items = items.filter((t) => t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.taskNumber.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  93 |         }
  94 |         if (category !== 'all') {
  95 |             items = items.filter((t) => t.category === category);
  96 |         }
  97 |         return items;
  98 |     }, [localTasks, query, category]);
  99 | 
 100 |     const columns = useMemo(() => {
 101 |         const map: Record<string, TaskRow[]> = {};
 102 |         for (const col of COLUMNS) map[col] = [];
 103 |         for (const t of filtered) {
 104 |             if (map[t.status]) map[t.status].push(t);
 105 |         }
 106 |         return map;
 107 |     }, [filtered]);
 108 | 
 109 |     const metrics = useMemo(() => {
 110 |         const open = localTasks.filter(isOpenTask);
 111 |         const overdue = localTasks.filter(isOverdue);
 112 |         const urgent = open.filter((task) => task.priority === 'urgent' || task.impact === 'critical');
 113 |         const blocked = open.filter((task) => task.status === 'blocked');
 114 |         const review = open.filter((task) => task.status === 'in_review');
 115 |         const completed = localTasks.filter((task) => task.status === 'completed');
 116 | 
 117 |         return { open, overdue, urgent, blocked, review, completed };
 118 |     }, [localTasks]);
 119 | 
 120 |     useEffect(() => {
 121 |         setFilter(activeFilter);
 122 |     }, [activeFilter]);
 123 | 
 124 |     useEffect(() => {
 125 |         setLocalTasks(tasks);
 126 |         setSelectedTask((current) => current ? tasks.find((task) => task.id === current.id) ?? current : null);
 127 |     }, [tasks]);
 128 | 
 129 |     const handleCreate = useCallback((event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
 130 |         event.preventDefault();
 131 |         setFormErrors({});
 132 |         router.post('/tasks', form, {
 133 |             preserveScroll: true,
 134 |             onSuccess: () => {
 135 |                 setCreateOpen(false);
 136 |                 setForm({ title: '', description: '', status: 'not_started', priority: 'medium', category: 'general_admin', due_date: '', assignee_ids: [], watcher_ids: [] });
 137 |                 toast.success('Task created.');
 138 |             },
 139 |             onError: (err) => {
 140 |                 setFormErrors(err);
 141 |                 toast.error('Please check form errors.');
 142 |             },
 143 |         });
 144 |     }, [form]);
 145 | 
 146 |     const replaceTask = useCallback((taskId: number, updater: (task: TaskRow) => TaskRow) => {
 147 |         setLocalTasks((current) => current.map((task) => task.id === taskId ? updater(task) : task));
 148 |         setSelectedTask((current) => current && current.id === taskId ? updater(current) : current);
 149 |     }, []);
 150 | 
 151 |     const updateStatus = useCallback((task: TaskRow, status: string) => {
 152 |         const previous = task;
 153 |         const nextStatus = status as TaskStatus;
 154 | 
 155 |         replaceTask(task.id, (current) => ({
 156 |             ...current,
 157 |             status: nextStatus,
 158 |             completedAt: nextStatus === 'completed' ? new Date().toISOString() : current.completedAt,
 159 |             progress: nextStatus === 'completed' ? 100 : current.progress,
 160 |         }));
 161 | 
 162 |         router.put(`/tasks/${task.id}/status`, { status }, {
 163 |             preserveScroll: true,
 164 |             preserveState: true,
 165 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Status updated.'),
 166 |             onError: () => {
 167 |                 replaceTask(task.id, () => previous);
 168 |                 toast.error('Status update failed.');
 169 |             },
 170 |         });
 171 |     }, [replaceTask]);
 172 | 
 173 |     const toggleChecklistItem = useCallback((taskId: number, itemId: number) => {
 174 |         const previous = localTasks.find((task) => task.id === taskId) ?? null;
 175 | 
 176 |         replaceTask(taskId, (task) => {
 177 |             const checklistItems = Array.isArray(task.checklistItems) ? task.checklistItems : [];
 178 |             const nextItems = checklistItems.map((item) => item.id === itemId ? { ...item, isDone: !item.isDone } : item);
 179 |             const progress = nextItems.length > 0
 180 |                 ? Math.round((nextItems.filter((item) => item.isDone).length / nextItems.length) * 100)
 181 |                 : 0;
 182 | 
 183 |             return {
 184 |                 ...task,
 185 |                 checklistItems: nextItems,
 186 |                 progress,
 187 |             };
 188 |         });
 189 | 
 190 |         router.put(`/tasks/${taskId}/checklist/${itemId}/toggle`, {}, {
 191 |             preserveScroll: true,
 192 |             preserveState: true,
 193 |             onError: () => {
 194 |                 if (previous) {
 195 |                     replaceTask(taskId, () => previous);
 196 |                 }
 197 | 
 198 |                 toast.error('Checklist update failed.');
 199 |             },
 200 |         });
 201 |     }, [localTasks, replaceTask]);
 202 | 
 203 |     const addChecklistItem = useCallback((taskId: number, label: string) => {
 204 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/checklist`, { label }, {
 205 |             preserveScroll: true,
 206 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Checklist item added.'),
 207 |             onError: () => toast.error('Checklist item could not be added.'),
 208 |         });
 209 |     }, []);
 210 | 
 211 |     const addComment = useCallback((taskId: number, body: string) => {
 212 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/comments`, { body, is_note: false }, {
 213 |             preserveScroll: true,
 214 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Comment added.'),
 215 |             onError: () => toast.error('Comment could not be added.'),
 216 |         });
 217 |     }, []);
 218 | 
 219 |     const uploadAttachment = useCallback((taskId: number, file: File) => {
 220 |         router.post(`/tasks/${taskId}/attachments`, { file }, {
 221 |             forceFormData: true,
 222 |             preserveScroll: true,
 223 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Attachment uploaded.'),
 224 |             onError: () => toast.error('Attachment upload failed.'),
 225 |         });
 226 |     }, []);
 227 | 
 228 |     return (
 229 |         <>
 230 |             <Head title="Tasks" />
 231 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.tasks" titleKey="nav.tasks" subtitleKey="Tasks and workflow management"
 232 |                 action={
 233 |                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
 234 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.visit('/task-requests')}>
 235 |                             Requests
 236 |                         </AppButton>
 237 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => setRequestOpen(true)}>
 238 |                             <Plus size={16} /> New request
 239 |                         </AppButton>
 240 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.visit('/workload')}>
 241 |                             Workload
 242 |                         </AppButton>
 243 |                         <AppButton variant="primary" onPress={() => { setFormErrors({}); setCreateOpen(true); }}>
 244 |                             <Plus size={16} /> Create task
 245 |                         </AppButton>
 246 |                     </div>
 247 |                 }
 248 |             >
 249 |                 <div className="crm-page">
 250 |                     <section className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
 251 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Open" value={metrics.open.length} helper="Active operational work" tone="blue" icon={Clock3} />
 252 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Urgent" value={metrics.urgent.length} helper="Urgent or critical impact" tone={metrics.urgent.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={Flame} />
 253 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Blocked" value={metrics.blocked.length} helper="Needs management attention" tone={metrics.blocked.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={ShieldAlert} />
 254 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Overdue" value={metrics.overdue.length} helper="Past due date" tone={metrics.overdue.length ? 'red' : 'green'} icon={AlertTriangle} />
 255 |                         <OperationsMetric label="Review" value={metrics.review.length} helper={`${metrics.completed.length} completed total`} tone="gold" icon={CheckCircle2} />
 256 |                     </section>
 257 | 
 258 |                     <TaskFilters
 259 |                         filter={filter}
 260 |                         category={category}
 261 |                         query={query}
 262 |                         viewMode={viewMode}
 263 |                         onFilterChange={(f) => { setFilter(f); router.visit(`/tasks?filter=${f}&category=${category}`, { preserveState: true }); }}
 264 |                         onCategoryChange={(c) => { setCategory(c); router.visit(`/tasks?filter=${filter}&category=${c}`, { preserveState: true }); }}
 265 |                         onQueryChange={setQuery}
 266 |                         onViewModeChange={setViewMode}
 267 |                     />
 268 | 
 269 |                     {viewMode === 'board' ? (
 270 |                         <TaskBoard columns={columns} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} />
 271 |                     ) : viewMode === 'calendar' ? (
 272 |                         <TaskCalendar tasks={filtered} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} />
 273 |                     ) : (
 274 |                         <TaskList tasks={filtered} columns={columns} onTaskClick={setSelectedTask} onStatusChange={updateStatus} />
 275 |                     )}
 276 | 
 277 |                     <TaskCreateDrawer
 278 |                         isOpen={createOpen}
 279 |                         users={users}
 280 |                         form={form}
 281 |                         formErrors={formErrors}
 282 |                         onOpenChange={(o) => { setCreateOpen(o); if (!o) setFormErrors({}); }}
 283 |                         onFormChange={setForm}
 284 |                         onSubmit={handleCreate}
 285 |                     />
 286 | 
 287 |                     <TaskDetailDrawer
 288 |                         task={selectedTask}
 289 |                         onClose={() => setSelectedTask(null)}
 290 |                         onComplete={(t) => updateStatus(t, 'completed')}
 291 |                         onChecklistToggle={toggleChecklistItem}
 292 |                         onChecklistAdd={addChecklistItem}
 293 |                         onCommentAdd={addComment}
 294 |                         onAttachmentUpload={uploadAttachment}
 295 |                     />
 296 | 
 297 |                     <TaskRequestCreateDrawer
 298 |                         isOpen={requestOpen}
 299 |                         requestTypes={taskRequestTypes}
 300 |                         requestTypeLabels={taskRequestTypeLabels}
 301 |                         options={taskRequestOptions}
 302 |                         onOpenChange={setRequestOpen}
 303 |                     />
 304 |                 </div>
 305 |             </AppShell>
 306 |         </>
 307 |     );
 308 | }

===== resources/js/pages/Notifications/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { Bell, CheckCheck, ListChecks, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { useMemo } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   6 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   7 | import type { NotificationRow } from '@/features/notifications/types';
   8 | 
   9 | type PageProps = {
  10 |     notifications: NotificationRow[];
  11 |     unreadCount: number;
  12 |     activeFilter: string;
  13 | };
  14 | 
  15 | const FILTERS = [
  16 |     { id: 'all', label: 'All' },
  17 |     { id: 'unread', label: 'Unread' },
  18 | ];
  19 | 
  20 | function notificationIcon(type: string) {
  21 |     switch (type) {
  22 |         case 'TaskNotification': return ListChecks;
  23 |         case 'ChatMessageNotification': return MessageSquare;
  24 |         default: return Bell;
  25 |     }
  26 | }
  27 | 
  28 | function notificationLink(notification: NotificationRow): string {
  29 |     const data = notification.data;
  30 |     if (data.task_id) return `/tasks?filter=all&category=all`;
  31 |     if (data.conversation_id) return `/inbox`;
  32 |     return '#';
  33 | }
  34 | 
  35 | export default function NotificationsIndex({ notifications, unreadCount, activeFilter }: PageProps) {
  36 |     const grouped = useMemo(() => {
  37 |         const groups: Record<string, NotificationRow[]> = { Today: [], 'This week': [], Earlier: [] };
  38 |         const now = new Date();
  39 |         for (const n of notifications) {
  40 |             const d = new Date(n.createdAt);
  41 |             const diffDays = Math.floor((now.getTime() - d.getTime()) / 86400000);
  42 |             if (diffDays === 0) groups.Today.push(n);
  43 |             else if (diffDays < 7) groups['This week'].push(n);
  44 |             else groups.Earlier.push(n);
  45 |         }
  46 |         return groups;
  47 |     }, [notifications]);
  48 | 
  49 |     function markAsRead(id: string) {
  50 |         router.post(`/notifications/${id}/read`, {}, {
  51 |             preserveScroll: true,
  52 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('Marked as read.'),
  53 |         });
  54 |     }
  55 | 
  56 |     function markAllAsRead() {
  57 |         router.post('/notifications/read-all', {}, {
  58 |             preserveScroll: true,
  59 |             onSuccess: () => toast.success('All marked as read.'),
  60 |         });
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 |     return (
  64 |         <>
  65 |             <Head title="Notifications" />
  66 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.notifications" titleKey="nav.notifications" subtitleKey="Task updates, chat messages, and system alerts"
  67 |                 action={
  68 |                     unreadCount > 0 ? (
  69 |                         <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={markAllAsRead}>
  70 |                             <CheckCheck size={16} /> Mark all read
  71 |                         </AppButton>
  72 |                     ) : undefined
  73 |                 }
  74 |             >
  75 |                 <div className="crm-page">
  76 |                     <section className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
  77 |                         {FILTERS.map((f) => (
  78 |                             <button key={f.id} type="button" onClick={() => router.visit(`/notifications?filter=${f.id}`, { preserveState: true })}
  79 |                                 className={`inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 text-xs font-semibold transition ${activeFilter === f.id ? 'border-[var(--crm-gold)] bg-[var(--crm-gold-soft)] text-[var(--crm-gold)]' : 'border-[var(--crm-border)] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--crm-text)]'}`}>
  80 |                                 {f.label}
  81 |                                 {f.id === 'unread' && unreadCount > 0 ? <span className="rounded-full bg-red-500 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] text-white">{unreadCount}</span> : null}
  82 |                             </button>
  83 |                         ))}
  84 |                         <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{unreadCount} unread</span>
  85 |                     </section>
  86 | 
  87 |                     <section className="space-y-6">
  88 |                         {Object.entries(grouped).map(([groupName, items]) => (
  89 |                             items.length > 0 ? (
  90 |                                 <div key={groupName}>
  91 |                                     <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{groupName}</h3>
  92 |                                     <div className="space-y-1">
  93 |                                         {items.map((notification) => {
  94 |                                             const Icon = notificationIcon(notification.type);
  95 |                                             return (
  96 |                                                 <div key={notification.id} className={`flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 transition ${notification.isRead ? 'border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)]' : 'border-[var(--crm-gold)]/20 bg-[color-mix(in_srgb,var(--crm-gold)_6%,transparent)]'}`}>
  97 |                                                     <div className={`flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${notification.isRead ? 'bg-[var(--crm-surface-2)] text-[var(--crm-muted)]' : 'bg-[var(--crm-gold-soft)] text-[var(--crm-gold)]'}`}>
  98 |                                                         <Icon size={15} />
  99 |                                                     </div>
 100 |                                                     <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
 101 |                                                         <p className="text-sm text-[var(--crm-text)]">
 102 |                                                             {typeof notification.data.description === 'string' ? notification.data.description : notification.type}
 103 |                                                         </p>
 104 |                                                         <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{notification.createdAtHuman}</p>
 105 |                                                     </div>
 106 |                                                     <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
 107 |                                                         {notification.data.task_id || notification.data.conversation_id ? (
 108 |                                                             <button type="button" onClick={() => router.visit(notificationLink(notification))}
 109 |                                                                 className="crm-action-button h-7 px-2 text-xs">View</button>
 110 |                                                         ) : null}
 111 |                                                         {!notification.isRead ? (
 112 |                                                             <button type="button" onClick={() => markAsRead(notification.id)}
 113 |                                                                 className="crm-action-button h-7 w-7 px-0" title="Mark as read">
 114 |                                                                 <CheckCheck size={12} />
 115 |                                                             </button>
 116 |                                                         ) : null}
 117 |                                                     </div>
 118 |                                                 </div>
 119 |                                             );
 120 |                                         })}
 121 |                                     </div>
 122 |                                 </div>
 123 |                             ) : null
 124 |                         ))}
 125 |                         {notifications.length === 0 ? (
 126 |                             <div className="py-16 text-center">
 127 |                                 <Bell size={36} className="mx-auto text-[var(--crm-muted)]" />
 128 |                                 <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-[var(--crm-text-muted])">No notifications yet</p>
 129 |                             </div>
 130 |                         ) : null}
 131 |                     </section>
 132 |                 </div>
 133 |             </AppShell>
 134 |         </>
 135 |     );
 136 | }

===== resources/js/pages/Inbox/Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { FormEvent, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   6 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   7 | import type { FormErrors } from '@/lib/formErrors';
   8 | import type { ChatUserOption, ConversationRow, MessageRow } from '@/features/chat/types';
   9 | import { ConversationList } from '@/features/inbox/components/ConversationList';
  10 | import { MessageThread } from '@/features/inbox/components/MessageThread';
  11 | import { NewConversationDrawer } from '@/features/inbox/components/NewConversationDrawer';
  12 | 
  13 | type PageProps = {
  14 |     conversations: ConversationRow[];
  15 |     users: ChatUserOption[];
  16 |     unreadCount: number;
  17 | };
  18 | 
  19 | export default function InboxIndex({ conversations, users, unreadCount }: PageProps) {
  20 |     const [selectedConv, setSelectedConv] = useState<ConversationRow | null>(null);
  21 |     const [messages, setMessages] = useState<MessageRow[]>([]);
  22 |     const [messageBody, setMessageBody] = useState('');
  23 |     const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  24 |     const [newConvOpen, setNewConvOpen] = useState(false);
  25 |     const [newConvForm, setNewConvForm] = useState({ user_id: '', subject: '' });
  26 |     const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  27 |     const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  28 | 
  29 |     const filteredConvs = useMemo(() => conversations.filter((c) => {
  30 |         if (!search.trim()) return true;
  31 |         const q = search.toLowerCase();
  32 |         const parts = Array.isArray(c.participants) ? c.participants : [];
  33 |         return parts.some((p) => p?.user?.name?.toLowerCase().includes(q)) || (c.subject || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
  34 |     }), [conversations, search]);
  35 | 
  36 |     function openConversation(conv: ConversationRow) {
  37 |         setSelectedConv(conv);
  38 |         setLoading(true);
  39 |         fetch(`/inbox/${conv.id}`)
  40 |             .then((r) => r.json())
  41 |             .then((data) => {
  42 |                 setMessages(data.messages || []);
  43 |             })
  44 |             .catch(() => toast.error('Failed to load messages'))
  45 |             .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  46 |     }
  47 | 
  48 |     function sendMessage() {
  49 |         if (!messageBody.trim() || !selectedConv) return;
  50 |         fetch(`/inbox/${selectedConv.id}/messages`, {
  51 |             method: 'POST',
  52 |             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': (window as any).csrfToken || '' },
  53 |             body: JSON.stringify({ body: messageBody }),
  54 |         })
  55 |             .then((r) => r.json())
  56 |             .then((msg) => {
  57 |                 setMessages((prev) => [...prev, msg]);
  58 |                 setMessageBody('');
  59 |             })
  60 |             .catch(() => toast.error('Failed to send message'));
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 |     function handleNewConv(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  64 |         event.preventDefault();
  65 |         setFormErrors({});
  66 |         router.post('/inbox', {
  67 |             user_ids: [Number(newConvForm.user_id)],
  68 |             type: 'direct',
  69 |             subject: newConvForm.subject || null,
  70 |         }, {
  71 |             preserveScroll: true,
  72 |             onSuccess: () => {
  73 |                 setNewConvOpen(false);
  74 |                 setNewConvForm({ user_id: '', subject: '' });
  75 |                 toast.success('Conversation created.');
  76 |             },
  77 |             onError: (err) => setFormErrors(err),
  78 |         });
  79 |     }
  80 | 
  81 |     return (
  82 |         <>
  83 |             <Head title="Inbox" />
  84 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.inbox" titleKey="nav.inbox" subtitleKey="Direct messages and context chats"
  85 |                 action={
  86 |                     <AppButton variant="primary" onPress={() => { setFormErrors({}); setNewConvOpen(true); }}>
  87 |                         <Plus size={16} /> New conversation
  88 |                     </AppButton>
  89 |                 }
  90 |             >
  91 |                 <section className="grid min-w-0 flex-1 grid-cols-[320px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)]" style={{ maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)' }}>
  92 |                     <ConversationList
  93 |                         conversations={filteredConvs}
  94 |                         selectedConvId={selectedConv?.id ?? null}
  95 |                         search={search}
  96 |                         onSearchChange={setSearch}
  97 |                         onSelect={openConversation}
  98 |                     />
  99 | 
 100 |                     <div className="flex flex-col">
 101 |                         <MessageThread
 102 |                             conversation={selectedConv}
 103 |                             messages={messages}
 104 |                             loading={loading}
 105 |                             messageBody={messageBody}
 106 |                             onMessageBodyChange={setMessageBody}
 107 |                             onSend={sendMessage}
 108 |                         />
 109 |                     </div>
 110 |                 </section>
 111 | 
 112 |                 <NewConversationDrawer
 113 |                     isOpen={newConvOpen}
 114 |                     users={users}
 115 |                     formErrors={formErrors}
 116 |                     form={newConvForm}
 117 |                     onOpenChange={(o) => { setNewConvOpen(o); if (!o) setFormErrors({}); }}
 118 |                     onFormChange={setNewConvForm}
 119 |                     onSubmit={handleNewConv}
 120 |                 />
 121 |             </AppShell>
 122 |         </>
 123 |     );
 124 | }

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\CalendarEventDrawer.tsx =====

   1 | import { FormEvent, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

  21 | 
  22 | type UserOption = { id: number; name: string; email: string };
  23 |

  26 |     onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;

  29 |     defaultStart?: string;

  31 | 
  32 | export function CalendarEventDrawer({ isOpen, onOpenChange, users, editEvent, defaultStart }: Props) {
  33 |     const isEdit = !!editEvent;
  34 |     const [form, setForm] = useState(() => initForm(editEvent, defaultStart));
  35 |     const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  36 | 
  37 |     const currentForm = useMemo(() => {
  38 |         if (!isOpen) return form;
  39 |         return form;
  40 |     }, [form, isOpen]);
  41 | 
  42 |     function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  43 |         e.preventDefault();
  44 |         setFormErrors({});
  45 | 
  46 |         if (isEdit) {
  47 |             router.put(`/calendar/events/${editEvent!.id}`, form, {
  48 |                 preserveScroll: true,
  49 |                 onSuccess: () => { onOpenChange(false); },
  50 |                 onError: (err) => { setFormErrors(err); },
  51 |             });
  52 |         } else {
  53 |             router.post('/calendar/events', form, {
  54 |                 preserveScroll: true,
  55 |                 onSuccess: () => { onOpenChange(false); resetForm(); },
  56 |                 onError: (err) => { setFormErrors(err); },
  57 |             });
  58 |         }
  59 |     }
  60 | 
  61 |     function resetForm() {
  62 |         setForm(initForm(undefined, undefined));
  63 |     }
  64 | 
  65 |     function handleClose(o: boolean) {
  66 |         if (!o) { resetForm(); setFormErrors({}); }
  67 |         onOpenChange(o);
  68 |     }
  69 | 
  70 |     return (
  71 |         <AppDrawer isOpen={isOpen} onOpenChange={handleClose}
  72 |             title={isEdit ? 'Edit event' : 'New event'}
  73 |             description={isEdit ? `#${editEvent?.eventNumber || ''}` : 'Create a new calendar event'}
  74 |             footer={<>
  75 |                 <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => handleClose(false)}>Cancel</AppButton>
  76 |                 <AppButton variant="primary" type="submit" form="calendar-event-form">
  77 |                     {isEdit ? 'Update' : 'Create'}
  78 |                 </AppButton>
  79 |             </>}>
  80 |             <form id="calendar-event-form" className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
  81 |                 <AppFormErrorSummary errors={formErrors} />
  82 | 
  83 |                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
  84 |                     <AppSelect label="Type" placeholder="Select type" selectedKey={currentForm.type}
  85 |                         onSelectionChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, type: (v as CalendarEventType) || 'task' }))}
  86 |                         options={Object.entries(EVENT_TYPE_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({
  87 |                             id, label,
  88 |                         }))} />
  89 | 
  90 |                     <AppSelect label="Priority" placeholder="Select" selectedKey={currentForm.priority}
  91 |                         onSelectionChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, priority: (v as CalendarEventPriority) || 'medium' }))}
  92 |                         options={Object.entries(EVENT_PRIORITY_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({ id, label }))} />
  93 |                 </div>
  94 | 
  95 |                 <AppTextField label="Title" placeholder="Event title" value={currentForm.title}
  96 |                     onChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, title: v }))}
  97 |                     error={firstError(formErrors, 'title')} />
  98 | 
  99 |                 <AppTextarea label="Description" placeholder="Event description" value={currentForm.description}
 100 |                     onChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, description: v }))} />
 101 | 
 102 |                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
 103 |                     <AppTextField label="Start" type="datetime-local" value={currentForm.startsAt}
 104 |                         onChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, startsAt: v }))}
 105 |                         error={firstError(formErrors, 'starts_at')} />
 106 |                     <AppTextField label="End" type="datetime-local" value={currentForm.endsAt}
 107 |                         onChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, endsAt: v }))}
 108 |                         error={firstError(formErrors, 'ends_at')} />
 109 |                 </div>
 110 | 
 111 |                 <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
 112 |                     <input type="checkbox" checked={currentForm.allDay}
 113 |                         onChange={(e) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, allDay: e.target.checked }))}
 114 |                         className="accent-[var(--crm-gold)]" />
 115 |                     All day
 116 |                 </label>
 117 | 
 118 |                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
 119 |                     <AppSelect label="Status" placeholder="Select" selectedKey={currentForm.status}
 120 |                         onSelectionChange={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, status: (v as CalendarEventStatus) || 'scheduled' }))}

 134 |                             return (

 136 |                                     className={`flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 transition ${currentForm.color === type ? 'ring-2 ring-[var(--crm-gold)] ring-offset-2 ring-offset-[var(--crm-elevated)]' : ''} ${borderClass || 'border-[var(--crm-border)]'} ${bgClass || 'bg-zinc-500/20'}`}
 137 |                                     title={EVENT_TYPE_LABELS[type as CalendarEventType]}>

 140 |                             );
 141 |                         })}
 142 |                     </div>
 143 |                 </div>
 144 |

 151 |                     {currentForm.participantIds.length > 0 ? (

 159 |                                             className="text-[var(--crm-muted)] hover:text-red-400"><X size={12} /></button>
 160 |                                     </span>

 162 |                             })}
 163 |                         </div>
 164 |                     ) : null}

 172 | }
 173 | 
 174 | function initForm(edit?: CalendarEventRow | null, defaultStart?: string) {
 175 |     if (edit) {
 176 |         return {
 177 |             type: edit.type,
 178 |             title: edit.title,
 179 |             description: edit.description || '',
 180 |             status: edit.status,
 181 |             priority: edit.priority,
 182 |             color: edit.color || edit.type,
 183 |             startsAt: edit.startsAt?.slice(0, 16) || '',
 184 |             endsAt: edit.endsAt?.slice(0, 16) || '',
 185 |             allDay: edit.allDay,
 186 |             timezone: edit.timezone,
 187 |             visibility: edit.visibility,
 188 |             ownerId: edit.owner?.id || null,
 189 |             clientId: edit.clientId,
 190 |             dossierId: edit.dossierId,
 191 |             dossierDocumentId: edit.dossierDocumentId,
 192 |             financeDocumentId: edit.financeDocumentId,
 193 |             contractId: edit.contractId,
 194 |             authorizationId: edit.authorizationId,
 195 |             archiveRecordId: edit.archiveRecordId,
 196 |             participantIds: (edit.participants || []).map((p) => p.userId),
 197 |             reminderOffset: (edit.reminders || [])[0]?.offsetMinutes ?? null,
 198 |         };
 199 |     }
 200 | 
 201 |     return {
 202 |         type: 'task' as CalendarEventType,
 203 |         title: '',
 204 |         description: '',
 205 |         status: 'scheduled' as CalendarEventStatus,
 206 |         priority: 'medium' as CalendarEventPriority,
 207 |         color: 'task',
 208 |         startsAt: defaultStart || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16),
 209 |         endsAt: '',
 210 |         allDay: false,
 211 |         timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone,
 212 |         visibility: 'assigned_users' as CalendarVisibility,
 213 |         ownerId: null,
 214 |         clientId: null,
 215 |         dossierId: null,
 216 |         dossierDocumentId: null,
 217 |         financeDocumentId: null,
 218 |         contractId: null,
 219 |         authorizationId: null,
 220 |         archiveRecordId: null,
 221 |         participantIds: [] as number[],
 222 |         reminderOffset: null as number | null,
 223 |     };
 224 | }

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\CalendarEventPill.tsx =====

   1 | import type { CalendarEventType } from '@/features/calendar/types';

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\CalendarRightPanel.tsx =====

   1 | import { CalendarDays, ListTodo, StickyNote, Bell, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | import type { CalendarEventRow, CalendarEventType } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   3 | import { EVENT_TYPE_CLASSES } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   4 | 
   5 | type Props = {
   6 |     events: CalendarEventRow[];
   7 |     onEventClick: (e: CalendarEventRow) => void;
   8 |     onCreateEvent: () => void;
   9 | };
  10 | 
  11 | export function CalendarRightPanel({ events, onEventClick, onCreateEvent }: Props) {
  12 |     const now = new Date();
  13 |     const todayStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  14 | 
  15 |     const dueToday = events.filter((e) => e.startsAt?.startsWith(todayStr)).slice(0, 8);
  16 |     const overdue = events.filter(
  17 |         (e) => e.startsAt < todayStr && e.status !== 'completed' && e.status !== 'cancelled',
  18 |     );
  19 |     const reminders = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'reminder').slice(0, 5);
  20 | 
  21 |     return (
  22 |         <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto">
  23 |             <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707] p-4">
  24 |                 <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
  25 |                     <CalendarDays size={14} className="text-[var(--crm-gold)]" />
  26 |                     <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--crm-gold)]">Today</span>
  27 |                     <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  28 |                         {now.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}
  29 |                     </span>
  30 |                 </div>
  31 |                 {dueToday.length === 0 ? (
  32 |                     <p className="py-4 text-center text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">Nothing scheduled today</p>
  33 |                 ) : (
  34 |                     <div className="space-y-1">
  35 |                         {dueToday.map((e) => (
  36 |                             <button key={e.id} type="button" onClick={() => onEventClick(e)}
  37 |                                 className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left transition hover:bg-white/5">
  38 |                                 <span className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${EVENT_TYPE_CLASSES[e.type]?.split(' ')[0] || 'bg-zinc-400'}`} />
  39 |                                 <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  40 |                                     <p className="truncate text-[11px] font-medium">{e.title}</p>
  41 |                                     <p className="text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  42 |                                         {e.allDay ? 'All day' : new Date(e.startsAt).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
  43 |                                     </p>
  44 |                                 </div>
  45 |                             </button>
  46 |                         ))}
  47 |                     </div>
  48 |                 )}
  49 |             </div>
  50 | 
  51 |             {reminders.length > 0 && (
  52 |                 <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707] p-4">
  53 |                     <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
  54 |                         <Bell size={14} className="text-purple-400" />
  55 |                         <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-400">Reminders</span>
  56 |                         <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{reminders.length}</span>
  57 |                     </div>
  58 |                     <div className="space-y-1">
  59 |                         {reminders.map((e) => (
  60 |                             <button key={e.id} type="button" onClick={() => onEventClick(e)}
  61 |                                 className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left transition hover:bg-white/5">
  62 |                                 <Bell size={12} className="shrink-0 text-purple-400/60" />
  63 |                                 <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[11px] font-medium">{e.title}</span>
  64 |                             </button>
  65 |                         ))}
  66 |                     </div>
  67 |                 </div>
  68 |             )}
  69 | 
  70 |             {overdue.length > 0 && (
  71 |                 <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707] p-4">
  72 |                     <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
  73 |                         <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-red-400" />
  74 |                         <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-400">Overdue</span>
  75 |                         <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{overdue.length}</span>
  76 |                     </div>
  77 |                     <div className="space-y-1">
  78 |                         {overdue.map((e) => (
  79 |                             <button key={e.id} type="button" onClick={() => onEventClick(e)}
  80 |                                 className="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left transition hover:bg-white/5">
  81 |                                 <span className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${EVENT_TYPE_CLASSES[e.type]?.split(' ')[0] || 'bg-zinc-400'}`} />
  82 |                                 <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[11px] font-medium">{e.title}</span>
  83 |                                 <span className="text-[10px] text-red-400/60">
  84 |                                     {Math.ceil((Date.now() - new Date(e.startsAt).getTime()) / 86400000)}d
  85 |                                 </span>
  86 |                             </button>
  87 |                         ))}
  88 |                     </div>
  89 |                 </div>
  90 |             )}
  91 | 
  92 |             <div className="rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707] p-4">
  93 |                 <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
  94 |                     <ListTodo size={14} className="text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]" />
  95 |                     <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">Quick actions</span>
  96 |                 </div>
  97 |                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
  98 |                     <button type="button" onClick={onCreateEvent}
  99 |                         className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-white/8 px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition hover:bg-white/5">
 100 |                         <CalendarDays size={13} className="text-[var(--crm-gold)]" /> New event
 101 |                     </button>
 102 |                     <button type="button" onClick={onCreateEvent}
 103 |                         className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-white/8 px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition hover:bg-white/5">
 104 |                         <StickyNote size={13} className="text-blue-400" /> New note
 105 |                     </button>
 106 |                 </div>
 107 |             </div>
 108 |         </div>
 109 |     );
 110 | }

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\CalendarTodayPanel.tsx =====

   1 | import type { CalendarEventRow } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   2 | import { EVENT_TYPE_CLASSES, EVENT_TYPE_LABELS } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   3 | 
   4 | type Props = {
   5 |     events: CalendarEventRow[];
   6 |     onEventClick: (event: CalendarEventRow) => void;
   7 | };
   8 | 
   9 | export function CalendarTodayPanel({ events, onEventClick }: Props) {
  10 |     const now = new Date();
  11 |     const todayStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  12 | 
  13 |     const overdue = events.filter(
  14 |         (e) => e.startsAt < todayStr && e.status !== 'completed' && e.status !== 'cancelled',
  15 |     );
  16 |     const dueToday = events.filter((e) => e.startsAt?.startsWith(todayStr));
  17 |     const upcoming = events.filter(
  18 |         (e) => e.startsAt > todayStr && e.status !== 'completed' && e.status !== 'cancelled',
  19 |     ).slice(0, 5);
  20 | 
  21 |     return (
  22 |         <div className="space-y-3">
  23 |             <Section title="Overdue" count={overdue.length} events={overdue} onEventClick={onEventClick} accent="text-red-400" />
  24 |             <Section title="Today" count={dueToday.length} events={dueToday} onEventClick={onEventClick} accent="text-[var(--crm-gold)]" />
  25 |             <Section title="Upcoming" count={upcoming.length} events={upcoming} onEventClick={onEventClick} accent="text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]" />
  26 |         </div>
  27 |     );
  28 | }
  29 | 
  30 | function Section({ title, count, events, onEventClick, accent }: {
  31 |     title: string;
  32 |     count: number;
  33 |     events: CalendarEventRow[];
  34 |     onEventClick: (e: CalendarEventRow) => void;
  35 |     accent: string;
  36 | }) {
  37 |     if (events.length === 0 && title !== 'Overdue') return null;
  38 | 
  39 |     return (
  40 |         <div>
  41 |             <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
  42 |                 <span className={`text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider ${accent}`}>{title}</span>
  43 |                 <span className="text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{count}</span>
  44 |             </div>
  45 |             {events.length === 0 ? (
  46 |                 <p className="py-2 text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-text-soft)]">None</p>
  47 |             ) : (
  48 |                 <div className="space-y-1">
  49 |                     {events.map((e) => (
  50 |                         <button key={e.id} type="button" onClick={() => onEventClick(e)}
  51 |                             className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left transition hover:bg-[var(--crm-surface-2)]">
  52 |                             <span className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${EVENT_TYPE_CLASSES[e.type]?.split(' ')[0] || 'bg-zinc-400'}`} />
  53 |                             <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[11px] font-medium">{e.title}</span>
  54 |                             {e.startsAt?.startsWith(todayStr) ? (
  55 |                                 <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-[var(--crm-text-soft)]">
  56 |                                     {new Date(e.startsAt).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
  57 |                                 </span>
  58 |                             ) : null}
  59 |                         </button>
  60 |                     ))}
  61 |                 </div>
  62 |             )}
  63 |         </div>
  64 |     );
  65 | }

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\CalendarToolbar.tsx =====

   1 | import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | 
   3 | type Props = {
   4 |     currentDate: Date;
   5 |     viewMode: 'dayGridMonth' | 'timeGridWeek' | 'timeGridDay';
   6 |     onViewModeChange: (v: Props['viewMode']) => void;
   7 |     onPrev: () => void;
   8 |     onNext: () => void;
   9 |     onToday: () => void;
  10 | };
  11 | 
  12 | export function CalendarToolbar({ currentDate, viewMode, onViewModeChange, onPrev, onNext, onToday }: Props) {
  13 |     const title = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  14 |         month: 'long',
  15 |         year: 'numeric',
  16 |         day: viewMode === 'timeGridDay' ? 'numeric' : undefined,
  17 |     }).format(currentDate);
  18 | 
  19 |     return (
  20 |         <div className="flex h-[54px] items-center justify-between border-b border-white/8 px-4">
  21 |             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  22 |                 <button type="button" onClick={onToday} className="h-7 rounded-lg border border-white/8 px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold text-white/70 transition hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white">Today</button>
  23 |                 <div className="flex items-center">
  24 |                     <button type="button" onClick={onPrev} className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-l-lg border border-white/8 text-white/40 transition hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"><ChevronLeft size={14} /></button>
  25 |                     <button type="button" onClick={onNext} className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-r-lg border-b border-r border-t border-white/8 text-white/40 transition hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"><ChevronRight size={14} /></button>
  26 |                 </div>
  27 |             </div>
  28 | 
  29 |             <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold">{title}</h2>
  30 | 
  31 |             <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-white/8 p-0.5">
  32 |                 {(['dayGridMonth', 'timeGridWeek', 'timeGridDay'] as const).map((v) => (
  33 |                     <button key={v} type="button" onClick={() => onViewModeChange(v)}
  34 |                         className={`h-7 rounded-md px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold transition ${viewMode === v ? 'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black' : 'text-white/50 hover:text-white'}`}>
  35 |                         {v === 'dayGridMonth' ? 'Month' : v === 'timeGridWeek' ? 'Week' : 'Day'}
  36 |                     </button>
  37 |                 ))}
  38 |             </div>
  39 |         </div>
  40 |     );

===== resources\js\features\calendar\components\MiniCalendar.tsx =====

   1 | import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | 
   3 | type Props = {
   4 |     currentDate: Date;
   5 |     onDateChange: (d: Date) => void;
   6 |     onDayClick: (d: Date) => void;
   7 | };
   8 | 
   9 | const DAYS = ['Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa', 'Su'];
  10 | 
  11 | export function MiniCalendar({ currentDate, onDateChange, onDayClick }: Props) {
  12 |     const year = currentDate.getFullYear();
  13 |     const month = currentDate.getMonth();
  14 | 
  15 |     const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
  16 |     const lastDay = new Date(year, month + 1, 0);
  17 |     const startOffset = (firstDay.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  18 |     const daysInMonth = lastDay.getDate();
  19 |     const daysInPrevMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  20 | 
  21 |     const cells: { day: number; isOutside: boolean }[] = [];
  22 |     for (let i = startOffset - 1; i >= 0; i--) cells.push({ day: daysInPrevMonth - i, isOutside: true });
  23 |     for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) cells.push({ day: d, isOutside: false });
  24 |     let nextDay = 1;
  25 |     while (cells.length % 7 !== 0) cells.push({ day: nextDay++, isOutside: true });
  26 | 
  27 |     const today = new Date();
  28 |     const todayStr = `${today.getFullYear()}-${String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  29 |     const currentStr = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  30 | 
  31 |     function prev() { onDateChange(new Date(year, month - 1, 1)); }
  32 |     function next() { onDateChange(new Date(year, month + 1, 1)); }
  33 | 
  34 |     return (
  35 |         <div className="select-none">
  36 |             <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
  37 |                 <button type="button" onClick={prev} className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded text-[var(--crm-muted)] hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"><ChevronLeft size={13} /></button>
  38 |                 <span className="text-xs font-semibold">{new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }).format(currentDate)}</span>
  39 |                 <button type="button" onClick={next} className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded text-[var(--crm-muted)] hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white"><ChevronRight size={13} /></button>
  40 |             </div>
  41 |             <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-0 text-center">
  42 |                 {DAYS.map((d) => <div key={d} className="py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{d}</div>)}
  43 |                 {cells.map((cell, i) => {
  44 |                     const m = cell.isOutside ? (cell.day > 15 ? month - 1 : month + 1) : month;
  45 |                     const y = cell.isOutside ? (cell.day > 15 ? (month === 0 ? year - 1 : year) : (month === 11 ? year + 1 : year)) : year;
  46 |                     const dateStr = `${y}-${String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(cell.day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  47 |                     const isToday = dateStr === todayStr;
  48 |                     return (
  49 |                         <button key={i} type="button" onClick={() => onDayClick(new Date(y, m, cell.day))}
  50 |                             className={`mx-auto flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[11px] font-medium transition hover:bg-white/10 ${isToday ? 'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black hover:brightness-110' : cell.isOutside ? 'text-white/20' : 'text-white/70 hover:text-white'}`}>
  51 |                             {cell.day}
  52 |                         </button>
  53 |                     );
  54 |                 })}
  55 |             </div>
  56 |         </div>
  57 |     );
  58 | }

===== resources\js\features\calendar\types.ts =====

   1 | export type CalendarEventType =
   2 |     | 'task' | 'note' | 'reminder' | 'meeting' | 'deadline'

   5 |

  83 | };
  84 | 
  85 | export type CalendarFormData = {
  86 |     type: CalendarEventType;
  87 |     title: string;
  88 |     description: string;
  89 |     status: CalendarEventStatus;
  90 |     priority: CalendarEventPriority;
  91 |     color: string;
  92 |     startsAt: string;
  93 |     endsAt: string;
  94 |     allDay: boolean;
  95 |     timezone: string;
  96 |     visibility: CalendarVisibility;
  97 |     ownerId: number | null;
