===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskCalendar.tsx =====

   1 | import type { TaskRow } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   2 | import { PRIORITY_COLORS, PRIORITY_LABELS, STATUS_COLORS, STATUS_LABELS } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   3 | 
   4 | type Props = {
   5 |     tasks: TaskRow[];
   6 |     onTaskClick: (task: TaskRow) => void;
   7 | };
   8 | 
   9 | export function TaskCalendar({ tasks, onTaskClick }: Props) {
  10 |     const now = new Date();
  11 |     const today = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  12 |     const weekEnd = new Date(now); weekEnd.setDate(now.getDate() + (7 - now.getDay()));
  13 |     const weekEndStr = weekEnd.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  14 |     const groups: { label: string; tasks: TaskRow[] }[] = [
  15 |         { label: 'Overdue', tasks: [] },
  16 |         { label: 'Today', tasks: [] },
  17 |         { label: 'This week', tasks: [] },
  18 |         { label: 'Upcoming', tasks: [] },
  19 |         { label: 'No date', tasks: [] },
  20 |     ];
  21 |     for (const t of tasks) {
  22 |         if (!t.dueDate) { groups[4].tasks.push(t); continue; }
  23 |         if (t.dueDate < today && t.status !== 'completed' && t.status !== 'cancelled') { groups[0].tasks.push(t); continue; }
  24 |         if (t.dueDate === today) { groups[1].tasks.push(t); continue; }
  25 |         if (t.dueDate <= weekEndStr) { groups[2].tasks.push(t); continue; }
  26 |         groups[3].tasks.push(t);
  27 |     }
  28 | 
  29 |     return (
  30 |         <section className="crm-panel overflow-hidden">
  31 |             <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--crm-border)]">
  32 |                 {groups.map((g) => (
  33 |                     <div key={g.label} className="px-4 py-3">
  34 |                         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
  35 |                             <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{g.label}</span>
  36 |                             <span className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-muted)]">({g.tasks.length})</span>
  37 |                         </div>
  38 |                         {g.tasks.length === 0 ? <p className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-soft)] py-1">None</p> : g.tasks.map((t) => (
  39 |                             <button key={t.id} type="button" onClick={() => onTaskClick(t)}
  40 |                                 className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)] px-3 py-2 text-left transition hover:bg-[var(--crm-surface-2)] mb-1">
  41 |                                 <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${STATUS_COLORS[t.status]}`}>{STATUS_LABELS[t.status]}</span>
  42 |                                 <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-semibold">{t.title}</span>
  43 |                                 <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${PRIORITY_COLORS[t.priority]}`}>{PRIORITY_LABELS[t.priority]}</span>
  44 |                                 {t.assignees && Array.isArray(t.assignees) && t.assignees.length > 0 ? (
  45 |                                     <span className="flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-[8px] font-bold text-black">{t.assignees[0].name.charAt(0)}</span>
  46 |                                 ) : null}
  47 |                             </button>
  48 |                         ))}
  49 |                     </div>
  50 |                 ))}
  51 |             </div>
  52 |         </section>
  53 |     );
  54 | }

===== resources\js\pages\Calendar\Index.tsx =====

   1 | import { Head, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
   3 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   4 | import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell';
   5 | import type { CalendarEventRow } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   6 | import { EVENT_TYPE_COLORS } from '@/features/calendar/types';
   7 | import { CalendarSidebar } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarSidebar';
   8 | import { CalendarToolbar } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarToolbar';
   9 | import { CalendarEventPill } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarEventPill';
  10 | import { CalendarEventDrawer } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarEventDrawer';
  11 | import { CalendarRightPanel } from '@/features/calendar/components/CalendarRightPanel';
  12 | import FullCalendar from '@fullcalendar/react';
  13 | import dayGridPlugin from '@fullcalendar/daygrid';
  14 | import timeGridPlugin from '@fullcalendar/timegrid';
  15 | import interactionPlugin from '@fullcalendar/interaction';
  16 | import type { EventClickArg, DateSelectArg, EventDropArg, EventResizeDoneArg } from '@fullcalendar/core';
  17 | 
  18 | type PageProps = {
  19 |     events: CalendarEventRow[];
  20 |     users: { id: number; name: string; email: string }[];
  21 | };
  22 | 
  23 | function toFC(event: CalendarEventRow) {
  24 |     const color = event.color || EVENT_TYPE_COLORS[event.type] || '#6b7280';
  25 |     return {
  26 |         id: String(event.id),
  27 |         title: event.title,
  28 |         start: event.startsAt,
  29 |         end: event.endsAt || undefined,
  30 |         allDay: event.allDay,
  31 |         backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  32 |         borderColor: 'transparent',
  33 |         textColor: color,
  34 |         extendedProps: {
  35 |             type: event.type,
  36 |             description: event.description,
  37 |             status: event.status,
  38 |             priority: event.priority,
  39 |             eventNumber: event.eventNumber,
  40 |         },
  41 |     };
  42 | }
  43 | 
  44 | export default function CalendarIndex({ events: _events, users }: PageProps) {
  45 |     const initialEvents = Array.isArray(_events) ? _events : [];
  46 |     const calendarRef = useRef<FullCalendar>(null);
  47 |     const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(new Date());
  48 |     const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'dayGridMonth' | 'timeGridWeek' | 'timeGridDay'>('dayGridMonth');
  49 |     const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  50 |     const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('');
  51 |     const [filterUserId, setFilterUserId] = useState('');
  52 |     const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  53 |     const [editEvent, setEditEvent] = useState<CalendarEventRow | undefined>(undefined);
  54 |     const [defaultStart, setDefaultStart] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  55 | 
  56 |     const fcEvents = useMemo(() => initialEvents.filter((e) => {
  57 |         if (search.trim()) {
  58 |             const q = search.toLowerCase();
  59 |             if (!e.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) && !(e.description || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
  60 |         }
  61 |         if (filterType && e.type !== filterType) return false;
  62 |         if (filterUserId) {
  63 |             const uid = Number(filterUserId);
  64 |             const parts = e.participants || [];
  65 |             if (!parts.some((p) => p.userId === uid) && e.createdBy?.id !== uid) return false;
  66 |         }
  67 |         return true;
  68 |     }).map(toFC), [initialEvents, search, filterType, filterUserId]);
  69 | 
  70 |     function handleViewChange(v: typeof viewMode) {
  71 |         setViewMode(v);
  72 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  73 |         if (api) api.changeView(v);
  74 |     }
  75 | 
  76 |     function handlePrev() {
  77 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  78 |         if (api) api.prev();
  79 |     }
  80 | 
  81 |     function handleNext() {
  82 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  83 |         if (api) api.next();
  84 |     }
  85 | 
  86 |     function handleToday() {
  87 |         const api = calendarRef.current?.getApi();
  88 |         if (api) api.today();
  89 |     }
  90 | 
  91 |     const handleDatesSet = useCallback((arg: { start: Date; end: Date; view: { currentStart: Date } }) => {
  92 |         setCurrentDate(arg.view.currentStart);
  93 |     }, []);
  94 | 
  95 |     function handleEventClick(arg: EventClickArg) {
  96 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
  97 |         const found = initialEvents.find((e) => e.id === id);
  98 |         if (found) {
  99 |             fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}`)
 100 |                 .then((r) => r.json())
 101 |                 .then((data) => {
 102 |                     setEditEvent(data.data || found);
 103 |                     setDefaultStart(undefined);
 104 |                     setDrawerOpen(true);
 105 |                 })
 106 |                 .catch(() => {
 107 |                     setEditEvent(found);
 108 |                     setDefaultStart(undefined);
 109 |                     setDrawerOpen(true);
 110 |                 });
 111 |         }
 112 |     }
 113 | 
 114 |     function handleSidebarEventClick(event: CalendarEventRow) {
 115 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${event.id}`)
 116 |             .then((r) => r.json())
 117 |             .then((data) => {
 118 |                 setEditEvent(data.data || event);
 119 |                 setDefaultStart(undefined);
 120 |                 setDrawerOpen(true);
 121 |             })
 122 |             .catch(() => {
 123 |                 setEditEvent(event);
 124 |                 setDefaultStart(undefined);
 125 |                 setDrawerOpen(true);
 126 |             });
 127 |     }
 128 | 
 129 |     function handleDateSelect(arg: DateSelectArg) {
 130 |         setEditEvent(undefined);
 131 |         setDefaultStart(arg.startStr);
 132 |         setDrawerOpen(true);
 133 |     }
 134 | 
 135 |     function openCreateDrawer() {
 136 |         setEditEvent(undefined);
 137 |         setDefaultStart(undefined);
 138 |         setDrawerOpen(true);
 139 |     }
 140 | 
 141 |     function handleEventDrop(arg: EventDropArg) {
 142 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
 143 |         const startStr = arg.event.start?.toISOString();
 144 |         const endStr = arg.event.end?.toISOString();
 145 | 
 146 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}/move`, {
 147 |             method: 'PUT',
 148 |             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': (window as any).csrfToken || '' },
 149 |             body: JSON.stringify({ starts_at: startStr, ends_at: endStr }),
 150 |         })
 151 |             .then((r) => {
 152 |                 if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Move failed');
 153 |                 toast.success('Event moved.');
 154 |             })
 155 |             .catch(() => {
 156 |                 arg.revert();
 157 |                 toast.error('Failed to move event.');
 158 |             });
 159 |     }
 160 | 
 161 |     function handleEventResize(arg: EventResizeDoneArg) {
 162 |         const id = Number(arg.event.id);
 163 |         const endStr = arg.event.end?.toISOString();
 164 | 
 165 |         fetch(`/calendar/events/${id}/resize`, {
 166 |             method: 'PUT',
 167 |             headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRF-TOKEN': (window as any).csrfToken || '' },
 168 |             body: JSON.stringify({ ends_at: endStr }),
 169 |         })
 170 |             .then((r) => {
 171 |                 if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Resize failed');
 172 |                 toast.success('Event resized.');
 173 |             })
 174 |             .catch(() => {
 175 |                 arg.revert();
 176 |                 toast.error('Failed to resize event.');
 177 |             });
 178 |     }
 179 | 
 180 |     const headerAction = (
 181 |         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
 182 |             <button type="button" onClick={handleToday} className="h-8 rounded-lg border border-white/8 px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white/70 transition hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white">Today</button>
 183 |             <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-white/8 p-0.5">
 184 |                 {(['dayGridMonth', 'timeGridWeek', 'timeGridDay'] as const).map((v) => (
 185 |                     <button key={v} type="button" onClick={() => handleViewChange(v)}
 186 |                         className={`h-7 rounded-md px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold transition ${viewMode === v ? 'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black' : 'text-white/50 hover:text-white'}`}>
 187 |                         {v === 'dayGridMonth' ? 'Month' : v === 'timeGridWeek' ? 'Week' : 'Day'}
 188 |                     </button>
 189 |                 ))}
 190 |             </div>
 191 |             <button type="button" onClick={openCreateDrawer} className="flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[var(--crm-gold)] px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-black transition hover:brightness-110">
 192 |                 <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"/><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"/></svg>
 193 |                 New event
 194 |             </button>
 195 |         </div>
 196 |     );
 197 | 
 198 |     return (
 199 |         <>
 200 |             <Head title="Calendar" />
 201 |             <AppShell eyebrowKey="nav.calendar" titleKey="nav.calendar" subtitleKey="Schedule tasks, notes, reminders, and follow-ups." action={headerAction}>
 202 |                 <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px] px-4 pt-4">
 203 |                     <div className="grid grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)_320px] gap-4 max-xl:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)] max-md:grid-cols-1">
 204 |                         <CalendarSidebar
 205 |                             search={search}
 206 |                             onSearchChange={setSearch}
 207 |                             filterType={filterType}
 208 |                             onFilterTypeChange={setFilterType}
 209 |                             filterUserId={filterUserId}
 210 |                             onFilterUserIdChange={setFilterUserId}
 211 |                             currentDate={currentDate}
 212 |                             onDateChange={(d) => { setCurrentDate(d); calendarRef.current?.getApi()?.gotoDate(d); }}
 213 |                             onDayClick={(d) => { calendarRef.current?.getApi()?.gotoDate(d); setViewMode('timeGridDay'); }}
 214 |                             users={users}
 215 |                             onCreateClick={openCreateDrawer}
 216 |                             events={initialEvents}
 217 |                             onEventClick={handleSidebarEventClick}
 218 |                         />
 219 | 
 220 |                         <div className="min-w-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-white/8 bg-[#070707]">
 221 |                             <CalendarToolbar
 222 |                                 currentDate={currentDate}
 223 |                                 viewMode={viewMode}
 224 |                                 onViewModeChange={handleViewChange}
 225 |                                 onPrev={handlePrev}
 226 |                                 onNext={handleNext}
 227 |                                 onToday={handleToday}
 228 |                             />
 229 |                             <div className="p-0">
 230 |                                 <FullCalendar
 231 |                                     ref={calendarRef}
 232 |                                     plugins={[dayGridPlugin, timeGridPlugin, interactionPlugin]}
 233 |                                     initialView={viewMode}
 234 |                                     events={fcEvents}
 235 |                                     eventContent={CalendarEventPill}
 236 |                                     eventClick={handleEventClick}
 237 |                                     selectable={true}
 238 |                                     select={handleDateSelect}
 239 |                                     datesSet={handleDatesSet}
 240 |                                     dayMaxEvents={3}
 241 |                                     height="auto"
 242 |                                     headerToolbar={false}
 243 |                                     firstDay={1}
 244 |                                     slotMinTime="07:00:00"
 245 |                                     slotMaxTime="20:00:00"
 246 |                                     allDaySlot={true}
 247 |                                     nowIndicator={true}
 248 |                                     editable={true}
 249 |                                     eventDrop={handleEventDrop}
 250 |                                     eventResize={handleEventResize}
 251 |                                     eventDurationEditable={true}
 252 |                                     eventStartEditable={true}
 253 |                                 />
 254 |                             </div>
 255 |                         </div>
 256 | 
 257 |                         <div className="max-xl:hidden">
 258 |                             <CalendarRightPanel
 259 |                                 events={initialEvents}
 260 |                                 onEventClick={handleSidebarEventClick}
 261 |                                 onCreateEvent={openCreateDrawer}
 262 |                             />
 263 |                         </div>
 264 |                     </div>
 265 |                 </div>
 266 |             </AppShell>
 267 | 
 268 |             <CalendarEventDrawer
 269 |                 isOpen={drawerOpen}
 270 |                 onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) { setDrawerOpen(false); setEditEvent(undefined); } }}
 271 |                 users={users}
 272 |                 editEvent={editEvent}
 273 |                 defaultStart={defaultStart}
 274 |             />
 275 |         </>
 276 |     );
 277 | }

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskBoard.tsx =====

   1 | import type { TaskRow } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   2 | import { COLUMNS, STATUS_COLORS, STATUS_LABELS } from '@/features/tasks/types';

   5 | type Props = {

   7 |     onTaskClick: (task: TaskRow) => void;
   8 | };
   9 | 
  10 | export function TaskBoard({ columns, onTaskClick }: Props) {
  11 |     return (
  12 |         <section className="grid min-w-0 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
  13 |             {COLUMNS.map((status) => (
  14 |                 <div key={status} className="crm-panel flex min-w-0 flex-col overflow-hidden">
  15 |                     <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-black/10 px-3 py-2.5">
  16 |                         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">

  18 |                                 {STATUS_LABELS[status]}
  19 |                             </span>
  20 |                             <span className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{columns[status]?.length || 0}</span>
  21 |                         </div>
  22 |                     </div>
  23 |                     <div className="space-y-2 p-2">
  24 |                         {(columns[status] || []).map((task) => (
  25 |                             <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} onClick={() => onTaskClick(task)} />
  26 |                         ))}
  27 |                         {(columns[status] || []).length === 0 ? (
  28 |                             <div className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-black/10 py-8 text-center text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  29 |                                 No tasks
  30 |                             </div>
  31 |                         ) : null}
  32 |                     </div>
  33 |                 </div>
  34 |             ))}
  35 |         </section>
  36 |     );

  43 |                                 <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${PRIORITY_COLORS[t.priority]}`}>{PRIORITY_LABELS[t.priority]}</span>
  44 |                                 {t.assignees && Array.isArray(t.assignees) && t.assignees.length > 0 ? (
  45 |                                     <span className="flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-[8px] font-bold text-black">{t.assignees[0].name.charAt(0)}</span>
  46 |                                 ) : null}
  47 |                             </button>
  48 |                         ))}
  49 |                     </div>
  50 |                 ))}
  51 |             </div>
  52 |         </section>
  53 |     );
  54 | }

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskCard.tsx =====

   1 | import { AlertTriangle, CalendarDays, CheckSquare, Link2, MessageSquare, Paperclip } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | import type { TaskRow } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   3 | import {
   4 |     CATEGORY_COLORS, CATEGORY_LABELS, IMPACT_COLORS, IMPACT_LABELS, PRIORITY_COLORS, PRIORITY_LABELS, TYPE_LABELS,
   5 | } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   6 | 
   7 | export function TaskCard({ task, onClick }: { task: TaskRow; onClick: () => void }) {
   8 |     const overdue = task.dueDate && new Date(task.dueDate) < new Date() && task.status !== 'completed' && task.status !== 'cancelled';
   9 |     const categoryClass = CATEGORY_COLORS[task.category] || CATEGORY_COLORS.general_admin;
  10 |     const checklistItems = Array.isArray(task.checklistItems) ? task.checklistItems : [];
  11 |     const checklistTotal = checklistItems.length;
  12 |     const checklistDone = checklistItems.filter((item) => item.isDone).length;
  13 |     const linkedRecord = task.dossier?.object || task.client?.name || null;
  14 | 
  15 |     return (
  16 |         <button type="button" onClick={onClick} className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)] p-2.5 text-left transition hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)] hover:bg-[var(--crm-elevated)]">
  17 |             <div className="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-1.5">
  18 |                 <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${categoryClass}`}>
  19 |                     {CATEGORY_LABELS[task.category] || task.category}
  20 |                 </span>
  21 |                 <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-1">
  22 |                     <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${IMPACT_COLORS[task.impact] || ''}`}>
  23 |                         {IMPACT_LABELS[task.impact] || task.impact}
  24 |                     </span>
  25 |                     <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${PRIORITY_COLORS[task.priority] || ''}`}>
  26 |                         {PRIORITY_LABELS[task.priority] || task.priority}
  27 |                     </span>
  28 |                 </div>
  29 |             </div>
  30 | 
  31 |             <p className="line-clamp-2 text-[13px] font-semibold leading-5 text-[var(--crm-text)]">{task.title}</p>
  32 |             <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">
  33 |                 {task.taskNumber} / {TYPE_LABELS[task.type] || task.type}
  34 |             </p>
  35 | 
  36 |             <div className="mt-2 grid gap-1 text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  37 |                 {task.dueDate ? (
  38 |                     <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${overdue ? 'text-red-400' : ''}`}>
  39 |                         <CalendarDays size={12} />
  40 |                         {task.dueDate}
  41 |                     </span>
  42 |                 ) : null}
  43 |                 {linkedRecord ? (
  44 |                     <span className="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">
  45 |                         <Link2 size={12} />
  46 |                         <span className="truncate">{linkedRecord}</span>
  47 |                     </span>
  48 |                 ) : null}
  49 |             </div>
  50 | 
  51 |             {task.status === 'blocked' && task.blockedReason ? (
  52 |                 <div className="mt-2 flex gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-red-400/20 bg-red-400/10 p-2 text-[11px] leading-4 text-red-200">
  53 |                     <AlertTriangle size={12} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
  54 |                     <span className="line-clamp-2">{task.blockedReason}</span>
  55 |                 </div>
  56 |             ) : null}
  57 | 
  58 |             <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
  59 |                 <div className="flex -space-x-1.5">
  60 |                     {Array.isArray(task.assignees) ? task.assignees.slice(0, 3).map((a) => (
  61 |                         <div key={a.id} className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--crm-surface)] bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-[9px] font-bold text-black">
  62 |                             {a.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
  63 |                         </div>
  64 |                     )) : null}
  65 |                     {Array.isArray(task.assignees) && task.assignees.length > 3 ? (
  66 |                         <div className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--crm-surface)] bg-[var(--crm-surface-2)] text-[9px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  67 |                             +{task.assignees.length - 3}
  68 |                         </div>
  69 |                     ) : null}
  70 |                 </div>
  71 |                 <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  72 |                     {checklistTotal > 0 ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><CheckSquare size={12} />{checklistDone}/{checklistTotal}</span> : null}
  73 |                     {task.commentsCount > 0 ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><MessageSquare size={12} />{task.commentsCount}</span> : null}
  74 |                     {task.attachmentsCount > 0 ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><Paperclip size={12} />{task.attachmentsCount}</span> : null}
  75 |                 </div>
  76 |             </div>
  77 | 
  78 |             {task.progress > 0 && task.status !== 'completed' ? (
  79 |                 <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--crm-surface-3)]">
  80 |                     <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-[var(--crm-gold)]" style={{ width: `${task.progress}%` }} />
  81 |                 </div>
  82 |             ) : null}
  83 |         </button>
  84 |     );
  85 | }

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskCreateDrawer.tsx =====

   1 | import { X } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | import { FormEvent } from 'react';
   3 | import { AppDrawer } from '@/components/ui/AppDrawer';
   4 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   5 | import { AppFormErrorSummary } from '@/components/ui/AppFormErrorSummary';
   6 | import { AppSelect } from '@/components/ui/AppSelect';
   7 | import { AppTextField } from '@/components/ui/AppTextField';
   8 | import { AppTextarea } from '@/components/ui/AppTextarea';
   9 | import type { FormErrors } from '@/lib/formErrors';
  10 | import { firstError } from '@/lib/formErrors';
  11 | import type { TaskCategory, TaskPriority, UserOption } from '@/features/tasks/types';
  12 | import { CATEGORY_LABELS, PRIORITY_LABELS } from '@/features/tasks/types';
  13 | 
  14 | type TaskForm = {
  15 |     title: string; description: string; status: string;
  16 |     priority: string; category: string;
  17 |     due_date: string; assignee_ids: number[]; watcher_ids: number[];
  18 | };
  19 | 
  20 | type Props = {
  21 |     isOpen: boolean;
  22 |     users: UserOption[];
  23 |     form: TaskForm;
  24 |     formErrors: FormErrors;
  25 |     onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
  26 |     onFormChange: (f: TaskForm) => void;
  27 |     onSubmit: (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
  28 | };
  29 | 
  30 | export function TaskCreateDrawer({ isOpen, users, form, formErrors, onOpenChange, onFormChange, onSubmit }: Props) {
  31 |     return (
  32 |         <AppDrawer isOpen={isOpen} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
  33 |             title="Create task" description="Create a new task with assignees and context."
  34 |             footer={<><AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={() => onOpenChange(false)}>Cancel</AppButton><AppButton variant="primary" type="submit" form="create-task-form">Create</AppButton></>}>
  35 |             <form id="create-task-form" className="space-y-5" onSubmit={onSubmit}>
  36 |                 <AppFormErrorSummary errors={formErrors} />
  37 |                 <AppTextField label="Title" placeholder="Task title" value={form.title} onChange={(v) => onFormChange({ ...form, title: v })} error={firstError(formErrors, 'title')} />
  38 |                 <AppTextarea label="Description" placeholder="Task description" value={form.description} onChange={(v) => onFormChange({ ...form, description: v })} />
  39 |                 <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
  40 |                     <AppSelect label="Priority" placeholder="Select" selectedKey={form.priority} onSelectionChange={(v) => onFormChange({ ...form, priority: v ? String(v) : 'medium' })}
  41 |                         options={['low', 'medium', 'high', 'urgent'].map((k) => ({ id: k, label: PRIORITY_LABELS[k as TaskPriority] }))} />
  42 |                     <AppSelect label="Category" placeholder="Select" selectedKey={form.category} onSelectionChange={(v) => onFormChange({ ...form, category: v ? String(v) : 'general_admin' })}
  43 |                         options={['documents', 'client_follow_up', 'contract', 'authorization', 'finance', 'archive', 'general_admin'].map((k) => ({ id: k, label: CATEGORY_LABELS[k as TaskCategory] }))} />
  44 |                 </div>
  45 |                 <AppTextField label="Due date" type="date" value={form.due_date} onChange={(v) => onFormChange({ ...form, due_date: v })} />
  46 |                 <AppSelect label="Assignee" placeholder="Select assignee" selectedKey={null} onSelectionChange={(v) => {
  47 |                     if (v && !form.assignee_ids.includes(Number(v))) onFormChange({ ...form, assignee_ids: [...form.assignee_ids, Number(v)] });
  48 |                 }} options={users.map((u) => ({ id: String(u.id), label: u.name }))} />
  49 |                 {form.assignee_ids.length > 0 ? (
  50 |                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
  51 |                         {form.assignee_ids.map((id) => {
  52 |                             const u = users.find((x) => x.id === id);
  53 |                             return u ? <span key={id} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs">
  54 |                                 {u.name}
  55 |                                 <button type="button" onClick={() => onFormChange({ ...form, assignee_ids: form.assignee_ids.filter((x) => x !== id) })} className="text-[var(--crm-muted)] hover:text-red-400"><X size={12} /></button>
  56 |                             </span> : null;
  57 |                         })}
  58 |                     </div>
  59 |                 ) : null}
  60 |             </form>
  61 |         </AppDrawer>
  62 |     );
  63 | }

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskDetailDrawer.tsx =====

   1 | import { CalendarDays, CheckCircle2, Clock3, ExternalLink, MessageSquare, Paperclip, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | import { FormEvent, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   3 | import { AppDrawer } from '@/components/ui/AppDrawer';
   4 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   5 | import { router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   6 | import type { TaskRow } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   7 | import {
   8 |     CATEGORY_COLORS, CATEGORY_LABELS, IMPACT_COLORS, IMPACT_LABELS, PRIORITY_COLORS, PRIORITY_LABELS,
   9 |     STATUS_COLORS, STATUS_LABELS, TYPE_LABELS,
  10 | } from '@/features/tasks/types';
  11 | 
  12 | type Props = {
  13 |     task: TaskRow | null;
  14 |     onClose: () => void;
  15 |     onComplete: (task: TaskRow) => void;
  16 |     onChecklistToggle: (taskId: number, itemId: number) => void;
  17 |     onChecklistAdd: (taskId: number, label: string) => void;
  18 |     onCommentAdd: (taskId: number, body: string) => void;
  19 |     onAttachmentUpload: (taskId: number, file: File) => void;
  20 | };
  21 | 
  22 | function TaskBadge({ children, className }: { children: string; className: string }) {
  23 |     return (
  24 |         <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${className}`}>
  25 |             {children}
  26 |         </span>
  27 |     );
  28 | }
  29 | 
  30 | function DetailLine({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string | number | null }) {
  31 |     if (value === null || value === '') return null;
  32 | 
  33 |     return (
  34 |         <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)] px-3 py-2">
  35 |             <p className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">{label}</p>
  36 |             <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text)]">{value}</p>
  37 |         </div>
  38 |     );
  39 | }
  40 | 
  41 | export function TaskDetailDrawer({
  42 |     task,
  43 |     onClose,
  44 |     onComplete,
  45 |     onChecklistToggle,
  46 |     onChecklistAdd,
  47 |     onCommentAdd,
  48 |     onAttachmentUpload,
  49 | }: Props) {
  50 |     const [checklistLabel, setChecklistLabel] = useState('');
  51 |     const [commentBody, setCommentBody] = useState('');
  52 |     const [attachment, setAttachment] = useState<File | null>(null);
  53 | 
  54 |     useEffect(() => {
  55 |         setChecklistLabel('');
  56 |         setCommentBody('');
  57 |         setAttachment(null);
  58 |     }, [task?.id]);
  59 | 
  60 |     const links = useMemo(() => {
  61 |         if (!task) return [];
  62 | 
  63 |         return [
  64 |             task.client ? { label: `Client: ${task.client.name}`, href: `/clients/${task.client.id}` } : null,
  65 |             task.dossier ? { label: `Dossier: ${task.dossier.number}`, href: `/dossiers/${task.dossier.id}` } : null,
  66 |             task.conversationId ? { label: 'Conversation', href: '/inbox' } : null,
  67 |         ].filter(Boolean) as { label: string; href: string }[];
  68 |     }, [task]);
  69 | 
  70 |     const submitChecklist = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
  71 |         event.preventDefault();
  72 |         if (!task || !checklistLabel.trim()) return;
  73 | 
  74 |         onChecklistAdd(task.id, checklistLabel.trim());
  75 |         setChecklistLabel('');
  76 |     };
  77 | 
  78 |     const submitComment = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
  79 |         event.preventDefault();
  80 |         if (!task || !commentBody.trim()) return;
  81 | 
  82 |         onCommentAdd(task.id, commentBody.trim());
  83 |         setCommentBody('');
  84 |     };
  85 | 
  86 |     const submitAttachment = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
  87 |         event.preventDefault();
  88 |         if (!task || !attachment) return;
  89 | 
  90 |         onAttachmentUpload(task.id, attachment);
  91 |         setAttachment(null);
  92 |     };
  93 | 
  94 |     const checklistItems = Array.isArray(task?.checklistItems) ? task.checklistItems : [];
  95 |     const doneCount = checklistItems.filter((item) => item.isDone).length;
  96 | 
  97 |     return (
  98 |         <AppDrawer isOpen={!!task} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) onClose(); }}
  99 |             title={task?.title || 'Task details'} description={task?.taskNumber || ''}
 100 |             footer={<>
 101 |                 <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={onClose}>Close</AppButton>
 102 |                 {task && task.status !== 'completed' ? (
 103 |                     <AppButton variant="primary" onPress={() => { if (task) onComplete(task); }}>
 104 |                         <CheckCircle2 size={14} /> Mark complete
 105 |                     </AppButton>
 106 |                 ) : null}
 107 |             </>}>
 108 |             {task ? (
 109 |                 <div className="space-y-4">
 110 |                     <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
 111 |                         <TaskBadge className={STATUS_COLORS[task.status]}>{STATUS_LABELS[task.status]}</TaskBadge>
 112 |                         <TaskBadge className={PRIORITY_COLORS[task.priority]}>{PRIORITY_LABELS[task.priority]}</TaskBadge>
 113 |                         <TaskBadge className={IMPACT_COLORS[task.impact]}>{IMPACT_LABELS[task.impact]}</TaskBadge>
 114 |                         <TaskBadge className={CATEGORY_COLORS[task.category]}>{CATEGORY_LABELS[task.category]}</TaskBadge>
 115 |                     </div>
 116 | 
 117 |                     <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
 118 |                         <DetailLine label="Type" value={TYPE_LABELS[task.type]} />
 119 |                         <DetailLine label="Progress" value={`${task.progress}%`} />
 120 |                         <DetailLine label="Start" value={task.startDate} />
 121 |                         <DetailLine label="Due" value={task.dueDate} />
 122 |                         <DetailLine label="Estimated" value={task.estimatedMinutes ? `${task.estimatedMinutes} min` : null} />
 123 |                         <DetailLine label="Actual" value={task.actualMinutes ? `${task.actualMinutes} min` : null} />
 124 |                     </div>
 125 | 
 126 |                     {links.length > 0 ? (
 127 |                         <section className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-3">
 128 |                             <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">Linked records</p>
 129 |                             <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
 130 |                                 {links.map((link) => (
 131 |                                     <button key={link.href + link.label} type="button" onClick={() => router.visit(link.href)}
 132 |                                         className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--crm-text)] transition hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)] hover:text-[var(--crm-gold)]">
 133 |                                         <ExternalLink size={13} /> {link.label}
 134 |                                     </button>
 135 |                                 ))}
 136 |                             </div>
 137 |                         </section>
 138 |                     ) : null}
 139 | 
 140 |                     {task.description ? (
 141 |                         <section>
 142 |                             <p className="mb-1 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">Description</p>
 143 |                             <p className="text-sm text-[var(--crm-text)]">{task.description}</p>
 144 |                         </section>
 145 |                     ) : null}
 146 | 
 147 |                     <section className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-3">
 148 |                         <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
 149 |                             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">Checklist ({doneCount}/{checklistItems.length})</p>
 150 |                             <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{task.progress}%</span>
 151 |                         </div>
 152 |                         {checklistItems.length > 0 ? (
 153 |                             <div className="space-y-1">
 154 |                                 {checklistItems.map((item) => (
 155 |                                     <label key={item.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] px-3 py-2 text-sm">
 156 |                                         <input type="checkbox" checked={item.isDone} onChange={() => {
 157 |                                             onChecklistToggle(task.id, item.id);
 158 |                                         }} className="accent-[var(--crm-gold)]" />
 159 |                                         <span className={item.isDone ? 'line-through text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]' : ''}>{item.label}</span>
 160 |                                     </label>
 161 |                                 ))}
 162 |                             </div>
 163 |                         ) : (
 164 |                             <p className="rounded-lg border border-dashed border-[var(--crm-border)] px-3 py-3 text-sm text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">No checklist items yet.</p>
 165 |                         )}
 166 |                         <form onSubmit={submitChecklist} className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
 167 |                             <input value={checklistLabel} onChange={(event) => setChecklistLabel(event.target.value)}
 168 |                                 placeholder="Add checklist item"
 169 |                                 className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-surface)] px-3 py-2 text-sm text-[var(--crm-text)] outline-none focus:border-[var(--crm-gold)]" />
 170 |                             <AppButton variant="secondary" type="submit"><Plus size={14} /> Add</AppButton>
 171 |                         </form>
 172 |                     </section>
 173 | 
 174 |                     <section className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-3">
 175 |                         <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">People</p>
 176 |                         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
 177 |                             {task.assignees.map((a) => (
 178 |                                 <span key={a.id} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold">
 179 |                                     <span className="flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-[8px] font-bold text-black">{a.name.charAt(0)}</span>
 180 |                                     {a.name}
 181 |                                 </span>
 182 |                             ))}
 183 |                             {task.watchers.map((watcher) => (
 184 |                                 <span key={`watcher-${watcher.id}`} className="rounded-full border border-[var(--crm-border)] px-2.5 py-1 font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
 185 |                                     Watching: {watcher.name}
 186 |                                 </span>
 187 |                             ))}
 188 |                             {task.assignees.length === 0 && task.watchers.length === 0 ? <span className="text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">No people assigned.</span> : null}
 189 |                         </div>
 190 |                     </section>
 191 | 
 192 |                     {task.dueDate ? (
 193 |                         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
 194 |                             <CalendarDays size={14} className="text-[var(--crm-muted)]" />
 195 |                             <span className={task.dueDate && new Date(task.dueDate) < new Date() && task.status !== 'completed' ? 'text-red-400 font-semibold' : ''}>
 196 |                                 Due: {task.dueDate}
 197 |                             </span>
 198 |                         </div>
 199 |                     ) : null}
 200 | 
 201 |                     <section className="rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-3">
 202 |                         <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[var(--crm-muted)]">Collaboration</p>
 203 |                         <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
 204 |                             <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] px-3 py-2">
 205 |                                 <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]"><MessageSquare size={13} /> Comments</p>
 206 |                                 <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-black text-[var(--crm-text)]">{task.commentsCount}</p>
 207 |                             </div>
 208 |                             <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] px-3 py-2">
 209 |                                 <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]"><Paperclip size={13} /> Attachments</p>
 210 |                                 <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-black text-[var(--crm-text)]">{task.attachmentsCount}</p>
 211 |                             </div>
 212 |                         </div>
 213 |                         <form onSubmit={submitComment} className="mt-3 space-y-2">
 214 |                             <textarea value={commentBody} onChange={(event) => setCommentBody(event.target.value)}
 215 |                                 placeholder="Write a comment or @mention someone"
 216 |                                 rows={3}

   4 | const FILTERS = [

  38 | export function TaskFilters({ filter, category, query, viewMode, onFilterChange, onCategoryChange, onQueryChange, onViewModeChange }: Props) {
  39 |     const [scopeOpen, setScopeOpen] = useState(false);
  40 |     const [moduleOpen, setModuleOpen] = useState(false);
  41 |     const chipClass = (active: boolean) => [

  46 |     ].join(' ');
  47 |     const activeFilter = FILTERS.find((item) => item.id === filter) ?? FILTERS[0];
  48 |     const activeCategory = CATEGORIES.find((item) => item.id === category) ?? CATEGORIES[0];
  49 | 
  50 |     return (
  51 |         <section className="crm-panel overflow-visible">
  52 |             <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 bg-black/10 p-3">

  54 |                     <Search size={14} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--crm-muted)]" />
  55 |                     <input value={query} onChange={(e) => onQueryChange(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search task, number, client, project..." className="crm-command-input h-9 w-full pl-8 pr-8 text-xs" />
  56 |                     {query ? <button type="button" onClick={() => onQueryChange('')} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--crm-muted)]"><X size={12} /></button> : null}
  57 |                 </div>
  58 | 
  59 |                 <div className="relative shrink-0">
  60 |                     <button
  61 |                         type="button"
  62 |                         onClick={() => { setScopeOpen((current) => !current); setModuleOpen(false); }}
  63 |                         className="inline-flex h-9 w-[178px] items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--crm-text)] transition hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)]"
  64 |                     >

  70 |                             {FILTERS.map((item) => (
  71 |                                 <button
  72 |                                     key={item.id}
  73 |                                     type="button"
  74 |                                     onClick={() => { onFilterChange(item.id); setScopeOpen(false); }}
  75 |                                     className={chipClass(filter === item.id)}
  76 |                                 >
  77 |                                     {item.label}
  78 |                                 </button>
  79 |                             ))}
  80 |                         </div>
  81 |                     ) : null}
  82 |                 </div>
  83 | 
  84 |                 <div className="relative shrink-0">
  85 |                     <button
  86 |                         type="button"
  87 |                         onClick={() => { setModuleOpen((current) => !current); setScopeOpen(false); }}
  88 |                         className="inline-flex h-9 w-[178px] items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] px-3 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--crm-text)] transition hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)]"
  89 |                     >
  90 |                         <span className="truncate">Module: {activeCategory.label}</span>
  91 |                         <ChevronDown size={13} className={moduleOpen ? 'rotate-180 transition' : 'transition'} />
  92 |                     </button>
  93 |                     {moduleOpen ? (
  94 |                         <div className="absolute left-0 top-10 z-50 grid w-[220px] gap-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-1 shadow-2xl shadow-black/40">
  95 |                             {CATEGORIES.map((item) => (
  96 |                                 <button
  97 |                                     key={item.id}
  98 |                                     type="button"
  99 |                                     onClick={() => { onCategoryChange(item.id); setModuleOpen(false); }}

 105 |                         </div>

'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black' : 'text-[var(--crm-text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--crm-text)]'}`}><Columns3 size={12} /> Board</button>

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskList.tsx =====

   1 | import { ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

   3 | import type { TaskRow } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   4 | import { COLUMNS, IMPACT_COLORS, IMPACT_LABELS, PRIORITY_COLORS, PRIORITY_LABELS, STATUS_COLORS, STATUS_LABELS, TYPE_LABELS, type TaskStatus } from '@/features/tasks/types';
   5 | 
   6 | type Props = {
   7 |     tasks: TaskRow[];

  10 |     onStatusChange: (task: TaskRow, status: string) => void;

  12 | 
  13 | function StatusMenu({
  14 |     task,
  15 |     onStatusChange,
  16 | }: {
  17 |     task: TaskRow;
  18 |     onStatusChange: (task: TaskRow, status: string) => void;
  19 | }) {
  20 |     const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  21 | 
  22 |     return (
  23 |         <div className="relative inline-flex" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
  24 |             <button
  25 |                 type="button"
  26 |                 onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
  27 |                 className={[
  28 |                     'inline-flex h-8 min-w-[128px] items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 text-xs font-bold transition',
  29 |                     'border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] text-[var(--crm-text)]',
  30 |                     'hover:border-[var(--crm-gold)] hover:bg-[var(--crm-surface)]',
  31 |                 ].join(' ')}
  32 |             >
  33 |                 <span className="truncate">{STATUS_LABELS[task.status]}</span>
  34 |                 <ChevronDown size={13} className={open ? 'rotate-180 transition' : 'transition'} />
  35 |             </button>
  36 | 
  37 |             {open ? (
  38 |                 <div className="absolute right-0 top-9 z-50 w-44 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[var(--crm-border)] bg-[var(--crm-elevated)] p-1 shadow-2xl shadow-black/40">
  39 |                     {COLUMNS.map((status) => (
  40 |                         <button
  41 |                             key={status}
  42 |                             type="button"
  43 |                             onClick={() => {
  44 |                                 setOpen(false);
  45 |                                 if (status !== task.status) {
  46 |                                     onStatusChange(task, status);
  47 |                                 }
  48 |                             }}
  49 |                             className={[
  50 |                                 'flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-xs font-semibold transition',
  51 |                                 status === task.status
  52 |                                     ? 'bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black'
  53 |                                     : 'text-[var(--crm-text)] hover:bg-[var(--crm-surface)]',
  54 |                             ].join(' ')}
  55 |                         >
  56 |                             <span>{STATUS_LABELS[status as TaskStatus]}</span>
  57 |                             <span className={`size-2 rounded-full border ${STATUS_COLORS[status]}`} />
  58 |                         </button>
  59 |                     ))}
  60 |                 </div>
  61 |             ) : null}
  62 |         </div>
  63 |     );
  64 | }
  65 | 
  66 | export function TaskList({ tasks, columns, onTaskClick, onStatusChange }: Props) {
  67 |     if (tasks.length === 0) {
  68 |         return (
  69 |             <section className="crm-panel overflow-hidden">
  70 |                 <div className="px-4 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">No tasks match the current filters.</div>
  71 |             </section>
  72 |         );
  73 |     }
  74 | 
  75 |     return (
  76 |         <section className="crm-panel overflow-hidden">
  77 |             <div className="overflow-x-auto">
  78 |                 <table className="crm-table">
  79 |                     <thead>
  80 |                         <tr>
  81 |                             <th>Task</th>
  82 |                             <th>Assigned to</th>
  83 |                             <th>Project</th>
  84 |                             <th>Type</th>
  85 |                             <th>Priority</th>
  86 |                             <th>Impact</th>
  87 |                             <th>Status</th>
  88 |                             <th>Due date</th>
  89 |                             <th>Updated</th>
  90 |                             <th className="text-right">Actions</th>
  91 |                         </tr>
  92 |                     </thead>
  93 |                     <tbody>
  94 |                         {tasks.map((task) => (
  95 |                             <tr key={task.id} onClick={() => onTaskClick(task)} className="cursor-pointer">
  96 |                                 <td>
  97 |                                     <p className="max-w-[260px] truncate font-semibold text-[var(--crm-text)]">{task.title}</p>
  98 |                                     <p className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{task.taskNumber}</p>
  99 |                                 </td>
 100 |                                 <td>
 101 |                                     {Array.isArray(task.assignees) && task.assignees.length > 0 ? (
 102 |                                         <div className="flex -space-x-1">
 103 |                                             {task.assignees.slice(0, 3).map((a) => (
 104 |                                                 <span key={a.id} className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--crm-surface)] bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-[9px] font-bold text-black" title={a.name}>
 105 |                                                     {a.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
 106 |                                                 </span>
 107 |                                             ))}
 108 |                                         </div>
 109 |                                     ) : <span className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">-</span>}
 110 |                                 </td>
 111 |                                 <td><span className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{task.dossier?.object || task.client?.name || '-'}</span></td>
 112 |                                 <td><span className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{TYPE_LABELS[task.type] || task.type}</span></td>
 113 |                                 <td><span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${PRIORITY_COLORS[task.priority]}`}>{PRIORITY_LABELS[task.priority]}</span></td>
 114 |                                 <td><span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${IMPACT_COLORS[task.impact]}`}>{IMPACT_LABELS[task.impact]}</span></td>
 115 |                                 <td><span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${STATUS_COLORS[task.status]}`}>{STATUS_LABELS[task.status]}</span></td>
 116 |                                 <td className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{task.dueDate || '-'}</td>
 117 |                                 <td className="text-xs text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{task.updatedAt || '-'}</td>
 118 |                                 <td>
 119 |                                     <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">

===== resources\js\features\tasks\components\TaskRequestCreateDrawer.tsx =====

   1 | import { router } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { FormEvent, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
   3 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   4 | import { AppButton } from '@/components/ui/AppButton';
   5 | import { AppDrawer } from '@/components/ui/AppDrawer';
   6 | import { AppFormErrorSummary } from '@/components/ui/AppFormErrorSummary';
   7 | import { AppSelect } from '@/components/ui/AppSelect';
   8 | import { AppTextarea } from '@/components/ui/AppTextarea';
   9 | import { AppTextField } from '@/components/ui/AppTextField';
  10 | import type { FormErrors } from '@/lib/formErrors';
  11 | import { firstError } from '@/lib/formErrors';
  12 | 
  13 | export type TaskRequestOption = {
  14 |     id: number;
  15 |     label: string;
  16 |     description?: string | null;
  17 |     clientId?: number | null;
  18 | };
  19 | 
  20 | export type TaskRequestOptions = {
  21 |     users: TaskRequestOption[];
  22 |     clients: TaskRequestOption[];
  23 |     dossiers: TaskRequestOption[];
  24 | };
  25 | 
  26 | type TaskRequestForm = {
  27 |     request_type: string;
  28 |     title: string;
  29 |     description: string;
  30 |     target_user_id: number | null;
  31 |     client_id: number | null;
  32 |     dossier_id: number | null;
  33 | };
  34 | 
  35 | type Props = {
  36 |     isOpen: boolean;
  37 |     requestTypes: string[];
  38 |     requestTypeLabels: Record<string, string>;
  39 |     options: TaskRequestOptions;
  40 |     onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  41 | };
  42 | 
  43 | const emptyForm: TaskRequestForm = {
  44 |     request_type: 'admin_help',
  45 |     title: '',
  46 |     description: '',
  47 |     target_user_id: null,
  48 |     client_id: null,
  49 |     dossier_id: null,
  50 | };
  51 | 
  52 | export function TaskRequestCreateDrawer({ isOpen, requestTypes, requestTypeLabels, options, onOpenChange }: Props) {
  53 |     const [form, setForm] = useState<TaskRequestForm>(emptyForm);
  54 |     const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  55 | 
  56 |     const dossierOptions = useMemo(() => {
  57 |         if (!form.client_id) return options.dossiers;
  58 | 
  59 |         return options.dossiers.filter((dossier) => dossier.clientId === form.client_id);
  60 |     }, [form.client_id, options.dossiers]);
  61 | 
  62 |     const close = () => {
  63 |         setErrors({});
  64 |         onOpenChange(false);
  65 |     };
  66 | 
  67 |     const updateDossier = (id: number | null) => {
  68 |         const dossier = options.dossiers.find((item) => item.id === id);
  69 |         setForm({
  70 |             ...form,
  71 |             dossier_id: id,
  72 |             client_id: dossier?.clientId ?? form.client_id,
  73 |         });
  74 |     };
  75 | 
  76 |     const submit = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
  77 |         event.preventDefault();
  78 |         setErrors({});
  79 | 
  80 |         router.post('/task-requests', form, {
  81 |             preserveScroll: true,
  82 |             onSuccess: () => {
  83 |                 setForm(emptyForm);
  84 |                 close();
  85 |                 toast.success('Task request submitted.');
  86 |             },
  87 |             onError: (validationErrors) => {
  88 |                 setErrors(validationErrors);
  89 |                 toast.error('Please check the task request form.');
  90 |             },
  91 |         });
  92 |     };
  93 | 
  94 |     return (
  95 |         <AppDrawer
  96 |             isOpen={isOpen}
  97 |             onOpenChange={(open) => (open ? onOpenChange(true) : close())}
  98 |             title="New task request"
  99 |             description="Create an operations request and link it to real CRM context."
 100 |             footer={
 101 |                 <>
 102 |                     <AppButton variant="secondary" onPress={close}>Cancel</AppButton>
 103 |                     <AppButton variant="primary" type="submit" form="task-request-create-form">Submit request</AppButton>
 104 |                 </>
 105 |             }
 106 |         >
 107 |             <form id="task-request-create-form" className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
 108 |                 <AppFormErrorSummary errors={errors} />
 109 |                 <AppSelect
 110 |                     label="Request type"
 111 |                     selectedKey={form.request_type}
 112 |                     onSelectionChange={(key) => setForm({ ...form, request_type: key ? String(key) : 'admin_help' })}
 113 |                     options={requestTypes.map((type) => ({ id: type, label: requestTypeLabels[type] ?? type }))}
 114 |                     error={firstError(errors, 'request_type')}
 115 |                     isRequired
 116 |                 />
 117 |                 <AppTextField
 118 |                     label="Title"
 119 |                     value={form.title}
 120 |                     onChange={(value) => setForm({ ...form, title: value })}
 121 |                     error={firstError(errors, 'title')}
 122 |                     placeholder="What needs to be done?"
 123 |                     isRequired
 124 |                 />
 125 |                 <AppTextarea
 126 |                     label="Description"
 127 |                     value={form.description}
 128 |                     onChange={(value) => setForm({ ...form, description: value })}
 129 |                     placeholder="Add useful details for the person who will handle it."
 130 |                 />
 131 |                 <AppSelect
 132 |                     label="Target user"
 133 |                     placeholder="Optional"
 134 |                     selectedKey={form.target_user_id ? String(form.target_user_id) : null}
 135 |                     onSelectionChange={(key) => setForm({ ...form, target_user_id: key ? Number(key) : null })}
 136 |                     options={options.users.map((user) => ({ id: String(user.id), label: user.label }))}
 137 |                 />
 138 |                 <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
 139 |                     <AppSelect
 140 |                         label="Client"
 141 |                         placeholder="Optional"
 142 |                         selectedKey={form.client_id ? String(form.client_id) : null}
 143 |                         onSelectionChange={(key) => setForm({ ...form, client_id: key ? Number(key) : null, dossier_id: null })}
 144 |                         options={options.clients.map((client) => ({ id: String(client.id), label: client.label }))}
 145 |                     />
 146 |                     <AppSelect
 147 |                         label="Dossier"
 148 |                         placeholder="Optional"
 149 |                         selectedKey={form.dossier_id ? String(form.dossier_id) : null}
 150 |                         onSelectionChange={(key) => updateDossier(key ? Number(key) : null)}
 151 |                         options={dossierOptions.map((dossier) => ({ id: String(dossier.id), label: dossier.label }))}
 152 |                     />
 153 |                 </div>
 154 |             </form>
 155 |         </AppDrawer>
 156 |     );
 157 | }

===== resources\js\features\tasks\types.ts =====

   1 | export type TaskStatus = 'backlog' | 'not_started' | 'in_progress' | 'waiting_client' | 'waiting_admin' | 'blocked' | 'in_review' | 'completed' | 'cancelled';
   2 | export type TaskPriority = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent';
   3 | export type TaskImpact = 'low' | 'normal' | 'high' | 'critical';
   4 | export type TaskType = 'general' | 'missing_document' | 'client_follow_up' | 'contract' | 'authorization' | 'finance' | 'archive' | 'review' | 'internal_admin';
   5 | export type TaskCategory = 'documents' | 'client_follow_up' | 'contract' | 'authorization' | 'finance' | 'archive' | 'general_admin';
   6 |

   1 | <?php

   8 | class CalendarProcessRemindersCommand extends Command
   9 | {

  11 |     protected $description = 'Process due calendar reminders and send in-app notifications';
  12 | 
  13 |     public function handle(CalendarReminderService $service): int
  14 |     {

  18 | 
  19 |         return Command::SUCCESS;
  20 |     }
  21 | }

===== app\Http\Controllers\CalendarEventController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Http\Requests\Calendar\StoreCalendarEventRequest;
   6 | use App\Http\Requests\Calendar\UpdateCalendarEventRequest;
   7 | use App\Http\Resources\CalendarEventResource;
   8 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   9 | use App\Services\Calendar\CalendarEventService;
  10 | use Carbon\Carbon;
  11 | use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
  12 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  13 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  14 | 
  15 | class CalendarEventController extends Controller
  16 | {
  17 |     public function __construct(
  18 |         protected CalendarEventService $service,
  19 |     ) {}
  20 | 
  21 |     public function store(StoreCalendarEventRequest $request): RedirectResponse
  22 |     {
  23 |         $this->authorize('create', CalendarEvent::class);
  24 | 
  25 |         $event = $this->service->create($request->validated(), $request->user());
  26 | 
  27 |         return redirect()->route('calendar.index')->with('success', 'Event created.');
  28 |     }
  29 | 
  30 |     public function show(CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): JsonResponse
  31 |     {
  32 |         $this->authorize('view', $calendarEvent);
  33 | 
  34 |         $calendarEvent->load([
  35 |             'creator', 'owner', 'participants.user', 'reminders',
  36 |             'task', 'dossier', 'client',
  37 |         ]);
  38 | 
  39 |         return response()->json(new CalendarEventResource($calendarEvent));
  40 |     }
  41 | 
  42 |     public function update(UpdateCalendarEventRequest $request, CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): RedirectResponse
  43 |     {
  44 |         $this->authorize('update', $calendarEvent);
  45 | 
  46 |         $this->service->update($calendarEvent, $request->validated(), $request->user());
  47 | 
  48 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Event updated.');
  49 |     }
  50 | 
  51 |     public function destroy(CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): RedirectResponse
  52 |     {
  53 |         $this->authorize('delete', $calendarEvent);
  54 | 
  55 |         $calendarEvent->delete();
  56 | 
  57 |         return redirect()->route('calendar.index')->with('success', 'Event deleted.');
  58 |     }
  59 | 
  60 |     public function move(Request $request, CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): JsonResponse
  61 |     {
  62 |         $this->authorize('move', $calendarEvent);
  63 | 
  64 |         $data = $request->validate([
  65 |             'starts_at' => ['required', 'date'],
  66 |             'ends_at' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:starts_at'],
  67 |         ]);
  68 | 
  69 |         $event = $this->service->move(
  70 |             $calendarEvent,
  71 |             Carbon::parse($data['starts_at']),
  72 |             isset($data['ends_at']) ? Carbon::parse($data['ends_at']) : null,
  73 |             $request->user(),
  74 |         );
  75 | 
  76 |         return response()->json(new CalendarEventResource($event));
  77 |     }
  78 | 
  79 |     public function resize(Request $request, CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): JsonResponse
  80 |     {
  81 |         $this->authorize('resize', $calendarEvent);
  82 | 
  83 |         $data = $request->validate([
  84 |             'ends_at' => ['required', 'date'],
  85 |         ]);
  86 | 
  87 |         $event = $this->service->resize(
  88 |             $calendarEvent,
  89 |             Carbon::parse($data['ends_at']),
  90 |             $request->user(),
  91 |         );
  92 | 
  93 |         return response()->json(new CalendarEventResource($event));
  94 |     }
  95 | 
  96 |     public function conflicts(CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): JsonResponse
  97 |     {
  98 |         $this->authorize('view', $calendarEvent);
  99 | 
 100 |         $participantIds = $calendarEvent->participants()->pluck('user_id')->all();
 101 | 
 102 |         return response()->json([
 103 |             'conflicts' => $this->service->conflicts($calendarEvent, $participantIds),
 104 |         ]);
 105 |     }
 106 | }

===== app\Http\Controllers\CalendarParticipantController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | use App\Services\Calendar\CalendarActivityService;
   8 | use App\Services\Calendar\CalendarNotificationService;
   9 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
  10 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  11 | 
  12 | class CalendarParticipantController extends Controller
  13 | {
  14 |     public function __construct(
  15 |         protected CalendarNotificationService $notificationService,
  16 |         protected CalendarActivityService $activityService,
  17 |     ) {}
  18 | 
  19 |     public function store(Request $request, CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): RedirectResponse
  20 |     {
  21 |         $this->authorize('update', $calendarEvent);
  22 | 
  23 |         $data = $request->validate([
  24 |             'user_id' => ['required', 'exists:users,id'],
  25 |             'role' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:owner,assignee,watcher,guest'],
  26 |         ]);
  27 | 
  28 |         $calendarEvent->participants()->firstOrCreate(
  29 |             ['user_id' => $data['user_id']],
  30 |             ['role' => $data['role'] ?? 'assignee'],
  31 |         );
  32 | 
  33 |         $user = User::find($data['user_id']);
  34 |         if ($user) {
  35 |             $this->notificationService->notifyAssigned($calendarEvent, $user);
  36 |         }
  37 | 
  38 |         $this->activityService->log($calendarEvent, $request->user()->id, 'participant_added', null, ['user_id' => $data['user_id']]);
  39 | 
  40 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Participant added.');
  41 |     }
  42 | 
  43 |     public function destroy(CalendarEvent $calendarEvent, User $user): RedirectResponse
  44 |     {
  45 |         $this->authorize('update', $calendarEvent);
  46 | 
  47 |         $calendarEvent->participants()->where('user_id', $user->id)->where('role', '!=', 'owner')->delete();
  48 | 
  49 |         $this->activityService->log($calendarEvent, request()->user()->id, 'participant_removed', null, ['user_id' => $user->id]);
  50 | 
  51 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Participant removed.');
  52 |     }
  53 | }

===== app\Http\Controllers\CalendarReminderController.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Controllers;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEventReminder;
   6 | use App\Services\Calendar\CalendarReminderService;
   7 | use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
   8 | use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
   9 | use Illuminate\Http\Request;
  10 | 
  11 | class CalendarReminderController extends Controller
  12 | {
  13 |     public function __construct(
  14 |         protected CalendarReminderService $service,
  15 |     ) {}
  16 | 
  17 |     public function store(Request $request, \App\Models\CalendarEvent $calendarEvent): RedirectResponse
  18 |     {
  19 |         $this->authorize('update', $calendarEvent);
  20 | 
  21 |         $data = $request->validate([
  22 |             'user_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:users,id'],
  23 |             'offset_minutes' => ['nullable', 'integer'],
  24 |             'remind_at' => ['nullable', 'date'],
  25 |             'channel' => ['nullable', 'string'],
  26 |         ]);
  27 | 
  28 |         $calendarEvent->reminders()->create([
  29 |             ...$data,
  30 |             'channel' => $data['channel'] ?? 'in_app',
  31 |         ]);
  32 | 
  33 |         return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Reminder added.');
  34 |     }
  35 | 
  36 |     public function snooze(Request $request, CalendarEventReminder $calendarReminder): JsonResponse
  37 |     {
  38 |         $this->authorize('snooze', $calendarReminder);
  39 | 
  40 |         $data = $request->validate(['minutes' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:1440']]);
  41 | 
  42 |         $reminder = $this->service->snooze($calendarReminder, $data['minutes'] ?? 5);
  43 | 
  44 |         return response()->json([
  45 |             'reminder' => new \App\Http\Resources\CalendarReminderResource($reminder),
  46 |         ]);
  47 |     }
  48 | 
  49 |     public function dismiss(CalendarEventReminder $calendarReminder): JsonResponse
  50 |     {
  51 |         $this->authorize('dismiss', $calendarReminder);
  52 | 
  53 |         $reminder = $this->service->dismiss($calendarReminder);
  54 | 
  55 |         return response()->json([
  56 |             'reminder' => new \App\Http\Resources\CalendarReminderResource($reminder),
  57 |         ]);
  58 |     }
  59 | }

===== app\Http\Requests\Calendar\StoreCalendarEventRequest.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Requests\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
   6 | 
   7 | class StoreCalendarEventRequest extends FormRequest
   8 | {
   9 |     public function authorize(): bool
  10 |     {
  11 |         return true;
  12 |     }
  13 | 
  14 |     public function rules(): array
  15 |     {
  16 |         return [
  17 |             'type' => ['required', 'string', 'in:task,note,reminder,meeting,deadline,client_follow_up,finance_follow_up,authorization_follow_up,contract_follow_up,archive_follow_up'],
  18 |             'title' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
  19 |             'description' => ['nullable', 'string'],
  20 |             'status' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:scheduled,in_progress,completed,cancelled,overdue'],
  21 |             'priority' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:low,medium,high,urgent'],
  22 |             'color' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:7'],
  23 |             'starts_at' => ['required', 'date'],
  24 |             'ends_at' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:starts_at'],
  25 |             'all_day' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
  26 |             'timezone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:64'],
  27 |             'visibility' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:private,assigned_users,team,admins'],
  28 |             'owner_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:users,id'],
  29 |             'task_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:tasks,id'],
  30 |             'client_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:clients,id'],
  31 |             'dossier_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:dossiers,id'],
  32 |             'dossier_document_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:dossier_documents,id'],
  33 |             'finance_document_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:finance_documents,id'],
  34 |             'contract_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:contracts,id'],
  35 |             'authorization_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:authorizations,id'],
  36 |             'archive_record_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:archive_records,id'],
  37 |             'participant_ids' => ['nullable', 'array'],
  38 |             'participant_ids.*' => ['integer', 'exists:users,id'],
  39 |             'reminder_offset' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:-1'],
  40 |         ];
  41 |     }
  42 | }

  26 |             'createdBy' => $this->whenLoaded('creator', fn () => new UserResource($this->creator)),

===== app\Http\Resources\CalendarParticipantResource.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Http\Resources;
