===== app\Policies\CalendarEventPolicy.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Policies;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | 
   8 | class CalendarEventPolicy
   9 | {
  10 |     public function viewAny(User $user): bool
  11 |     {
  12 |         return true;
  13 |     }
  14 | 
  15 |     public function view(User $user, CalendarEvent $event): bool
  16 |     {
  17 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  18 |         if ($event->visibility === 'admins') return false;
  19 |         if ($user->id === $event->created_by) return true;
  20 |         if ($event->visibility === 'team') return true;
  21 |         if ($event->visibility === 'assigned_users') {
  22 |             return $event->participants()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists();
  23 |         }
  24 |         return $user->id === $event->created_by;
  25 |     }
  26 | 
  27 |     public function create(User $user): bool
  28 |     {
  29 |         return true;
  30 |     }
  31 | 
  32 |     public function update(User $user, CalendarEvent $event): bool
  33 |     {
  34 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  35 |         if ($user->id === $event->created_by) return true;
  36 |         if ($event->participants()->where('user_id', $user->id)->where('role', 'owner')->exists()) return true;
  37 |         return false;
  38 |     }
  39 | 
  40 |     public function delete(User $user, CalendarEvent $event): bool
  41 |     {
  42 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  43 |         return $user->id === $event->created_by;
  44 |     }
  45 | 
  46 |     public function move(User $user, CalendarEvent $event): bool
  47 |     {
  48 |         return $this->update($user, $event);
  49 |     }
  50 | 
  51 |     public function resize(User $user, CalendarEvent $event): bool
  52 |     {
  53 |         return $this->update($user, $event);
  54 |     }
  55 | }

===== app\Policies\CalendarReminderPolicy.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Policies;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEventReminder;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | 
   8 | class CalendarReminderPolicy
   9 | {
  10 |     public function viewAny(User $user): bool
  11 |     {
  12 |         return true;
  13 |     }
  14 | 
  15 |     public function view(User $user, CalendarEventReminder $reminder): bool
  16 |     {
  17 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  18 |         return $reminder->user_id === $user->id || $reminder->event->created_by === $user->id;
  19 |     }
  20 | 
  21 |     public function create(User $user): bool
  22 |     {
  23 |         return true;
  24 |     }
  25 | 
  26 |     public function update(User $user, CalendarEventReminder $reminder): bool
  27 |     {
  28 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  29 |         return $reminder->user_id === $user->id || $reminder->event->created_by === $user->id;
  30 |     }
  31 | 
  32 |     public function delete(User $user, CalendarEventReminder $reminder): bool
  33 |     {
  34 |         if ($user->can('admin')) return true;
  35 |         return $reminder->user_id === $user->id || $reminder->event->created_by === $user->id;
  36 |     }
  37 | 
  38 |     public function snooze(User $user, CalendarEventReminder $reminder): bool
  39 |     {
  40 |         return $reminder->user_id === $user->id;
  41 |     }
  42 | 
  43 |     public function dismiss(User $user, CalendarEventReminder $reminder): bool
  44 |     {
  45 |         return $reminder->user_id === $user->id;
  46 |     }
  47 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarConflictService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use Carbon\Carbon;
   7 | use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
   8 | 
   9 | class CalendarConflictService
  10 | {
  11 |     public function check(CalendarEvent $event, array $participantIds): Collection
  12 |     {
  13 |         if (!$event->starts_at || !$event->ends_at) {
  14 |             return collect();
  15 |         }
  16 | 
  17 |         $start = Carbon::parse($event->starts_at);
  18 |         $end = Carbon::parse($event->ends_at);
  19 | 
  20 |         $conflicts = CalendarEvent::where('id', '!=', $event->id)
  21 |             ->where(function ($q) use ($start, $end) {
  22 |                 $q->whereBetween('starts_at', [$start, $end])
  23 |                   ->orWhereBetween('ends_at', [$start, $end])
  24 |                   ->orWhere(function ($q2) use ($start, $end) {
  25 |                       $q2->where('starts_at', '<=', $start)
  26 |                          ->where('ends_at', '>=', $end);
  27 |                   });
  28 |             })
  29 |             ->whereHas('participants', fn ($q) => $q->whereIn('user_id', $participantIds))
  30 |             ->whereNotIn('status', ['cancelled', 'completed'])
  31 |             ->with(['participants.user'])
  32 |             ->get();
  33 | 
  34 |         return $conflicts->map(fn ($conflict) => [
  35 |             'event_id' => $conflict->id,
  36 |             'title' => $conflict->title,
  37 |             'starts_at' => $conflict->starts_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
  38 |             'ends_at' => $conflict->ends_at?->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
  39 |             'participants' => $conflict->participants->pluck('user.name')->filter()->values(),
  40 |         ]);
  41 |     }
  42 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarEventService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | use Carbon\Carbon;
   8 | use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
   9 | 
  10 | class CalendarEventService
  11 | {
  12 |     public function __construct(
  13 |         protected CalendarNumberService $numberService,
  14 |         protected CalendarActivityService $activityService,
  15 |         protected CalendarNotificationService $notificationService,
  16 |         protected CalendarTaskSyncService $taskSyncService,
  17 |         protected CalendarConflictService $conflictService,
  18 |         protected CalendarRecurrenceService $recurrenceService,
  19 |     ) {}
  20 | 
  21 |     public function indexPayload(User $user, array $filters): array
  22 |     {
  23 |         $query = CalendarEvent::with([
  24 |             'creator', 'owner', 'participants.user', 'reminders',
  25 |         ])->where(function ($q) use ($user) {
  26 |             $q->where('created_by', $user->id)
  27 |               ->orWhereHas('participants', fn ($p) => $p->where('user_id', $user->id))
  28 |               ->orWhere('visibility', 'team')
  29 |               ->orWhere('visibility', 'admins');
  30 |         });
  31 | 
  32 |         if ($user->cannot('admin')) {
  33 |             $query->where(function ($q) use ($user) {
  34 |                 $q->where('visibility', '!=', 'admins')
  35 |                   ->orWhere('created_by', $user->id);
  36 |             });
  37 |         }
  38 | 
  39 |         if (!empty($filters['type'])) {
  40 |             $query->where('type', $filters['type']);
  41 |         }
  42 | 
  43 |         if (!empty($filters['status'])) {
  44 |             $query->where('status', $filters['status']);
  45 |         }
  46 | 
  47 |         if (!empty($filters['user_id'])) {
  48 |             $query->whereHas('participants', fn ($q) => $q->where('user_id', $filters['user_id']));
  49 |         }
  50 | 
  51 |         if (!empty($filters['start'])) {
  52 |             $query->where('starts_at', '>=', Carbon::parse($filters['start']));
  53 |         }
  54 | 
  55 |         if (!empty($filters['end'])) {
  56 |             $query->where('starts_at', '<=', Carbon::parse($filters['end']));
  57 |         }
  58 | 
  59 |         $events = $query->orderBy('starts_at')->get();
  60 | 
  61 |         return [
  62 |             'events' => \App\Http\Resources\CalendarEventResource::collection($events)->resolve(),
  63 |             'users' => User::orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name', 'email']),
  64 |         ];
  65 |     }
  66 | 
  67 |     public function create(array $data, User $user): CalendarEvent
  68 |     {
  69 |         $participantIds = $data['participant_ids'] ?? [];
  70 |         $reminderOffset = $data['reminder_offset'] ?? null;
  71 |         unset($data['participant_ids'], $data['reminder_offset']);
  72 | 
  73 |         $event = CalendarEvent::create([
  74 |             ...$data,
  75 |             'event_number' => $this->numberService->generate(),
  76 |             'created_by' => $user->id,
  77 |         ]);
  78 | 
  79 |         if ($participantIds) {
  80 |             $event->participants()->createMany(
  81 |                 collect($participantIds)->map(fn ($id) => [
  82 |                     'user_id' => $id,
  83 |                     'role' => $id === $user->id ? 'owner' : 'assignee',
  84 |                 ])->all()
  85 |             );
  86 |         }
  87 | 
  88 |         if (!$event->participants()->where('user_id', $user->id)->exists()) {
  89 |             $event->participants()->create([
  90 |                 'user_id' => $user->id,
  91 |                 'role' => 'owner',
  92 |             ]);
  93 |         }
  94 | 
  95 |         if ($reminderOffset !== null) {
  96 |             $event->reminders()->create([
  97 |                 'user_id' => $user->id,
  98 |                 'offset_minutes' => $reminderOffset,
  99 |                 'channel' => 'in_app',
 100 |             ]);
 101 |         }
 102 | 
 103 |         $this->activityService->log($event, $user->id, 'created');
 104 | 
 105 |         $event->load('participants.user', 'reminders');
 106 | 
 107 |         if ($event->type === 'task') {
 108 |             $this->taskSyncService->createTaskFromEvent($event, $user);
 109 |         }
 110 | 
 111 |         if ($event->recurrences()->exists()) {
 112 |             $this->recurrenceService->apply($event);
 113 |         }
 114 | 
 115 |         return $event->refresh();
 116 |     }
 117 | 
 118 |     public function update(CalendarEvent $event, array $data, User $user): CalendarEvent
 119 |     {
 120 |         $old = $event->replicate();
 121 |         $participantIds = $data['participant_ids'] ?? null;
 122 |         $reminderOffset = $data['reminder_offset'] ?? null;
 123 |         unset($data['participant_ids'], $data['reminder_offset']);
 124 | 
 125 |         $event->update($data);
 126 | 
 127 |         if (is_array($participantIds)) {
 128 |             $event->participants()->whereNotIn('role', ['owner'])->delete();
 129 |             foreach ($participantIds as $id) {
 130 |                 $event->participants()->firstOrCreate(
 131 |                     ['user_id' => $id],
 132 |                     ['role' => 'assignee']
 133 |                 );
 134 |             }
 135 |         }
 136 | 
 137 |         if ($reminderOffset !== null) {
 138 |             $event->reminders()->where('user_id', $user->id)->delete();
 139 |             $event->reminders()->create([
 140 |                 'user_id' => $user->id,
 141 |                 'offset_minutes' => $reminderOffset,
 142 |                 'channel' => 'in_app',
 143 |             ]);
 144 |         }
 145 | 
 146 |         $changes = collect($data)
 147 |             ->filter(fn ($value, $key) => $old->$key != $value)
 148 |             ->keys()
 149 |             ->all();
 150 | 
 151 |         if ($changes) {
 152 |             $this->activityService->log($event, $user->id, 'updated', null, ['changes' => $changes]);
 153 | 
 154 |             if (in_array('starts_at', $changes) || in_array('ends_at', $changes)) {
 155 |                 $event->participants()->with('user')->get()->each(function ($p) use ($event) {
 156 |                     $this->notificationService->notifyDateChanged($event, $p->user);
 157 |                 });
 158 |             }
 159 | 
 160 |             if (in_array('starts_at', $changes) || in_array('ends_at', $changes)) {
 161 |                 $this->taskSyncService->syncEventDatesToTask($event);
 162 |             }
 163 |         }
 164 | 
 165 |         return $event->fresh()->load(['participants.user', 'reminders']);
 166 |     }
 167 | 
 168 |     public function move(CalendarEvent $event, Carbon $newStart, ?Carbon $newEnd, User $user): CalendarEvent
 169 |     {
 170 |         $duration = $event->ends_at ? $event->starts_at->diffInMinutes($event->ends_at) : null;
 171 | 
 172 |         $event->update([
 173 |             'starts_at' => $newStart,
 174 |             'ends_at' => $newEnd ?? ($duration ? (clone $newStart)->addMinutes($duration) : null),
 175 |         ]);
 176 | 
 177 |         $this->activityService->log($event, $user->id, 'moved', [
 178 |             'starts_at' => $event->getOriginal('starts_at')?->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 179 |             'ends_at' => $event->getOriginal('ends_at')?->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 180 |         ], [
 181 |             'starts_at' => $newStart->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 182 |             'ends_at' => ($newEnd ?? ($duration ? (clone $newStart)->addMinutes($duration) : null))?->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 183 |         ]);
 184 | 
 185 |         $this->taskSyncService->syncEventDatesToTask($event);
 186 | 
 187 |         $event->participants()->with('user')->get()->each(function ($p) use ($event) {
 188 |             $this->notificationService->notifyDateChanged($event, $p->user);
 189 |         });
 190 | 
 191 |         return $event->fresh();
 192 |     }
 193 | 
 194 |     public function resize(CalendarEvent $event, Carbon $newEnd, User $user): CalendarEvent
 195 |     {
 196 |         $event->update(['ends_at' => $newEnd]);
 197 | 
 198 |         $this->activityService->log($event, $user->id, 'resized', [
 199 |             'ends_at' => $event->getOriginal('ends_at')?->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 200 |         ], [
 201 |             'ends_at' => $newEnd->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
 202 |         ]);
 203 | 
 204 |         return $event->fresh();
 205 |     }
 206 | 
 207 |     public function conflicts(CalendarEvent $event, array $participantIds): Collection
 208 |     {
 209 |         return $this->conflictService->check($event, $participantIds);
 210 |     }
 211 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarNumberService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | 
   7 | class CalendarNumberService
   8 | {
   9 |     public function generate(): string
  10 |     {
  11 |         $prefix = 'CAL-';
  12 |         $last = CalendarEvent::withTrashed()->where('event_number', 'like', $prefix . date('Y') . '-%')
  13 |             ->orderBy('id', 'desc')->value('event_number');
  14 | 
  15 |         if ($last) {
  16 |             $num = (int) substr($last, strrpos($last, '-') + 1) + 1;
  17 |         } else {
  18 |             $num = 1;
  19 |         }
  20 | 
  21 |         return $prefix . date('Y') . '-' . str_pad((string) $num, 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
  22 |     }
  23 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarRecurrenceService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use App\Models\CalendarEventRecurrence;
   7 | use Carbon\Carbon;
   8 | 
   9 | class CalendarRecurrenceService
  10 | {
  11 |     public function apply(CalendarEvent $event): void
  12 |     {
  13 |         $recurrence = $event->recurrences()->first();
  14 |         if (!$recurrence) return;
  15 | 
  16 |         $dates = $this->generateDates($recurrence, $event->starts_at);
  17 |         $base = $event->replicate();
  18 |         $duration = $event->ends_at ? $event->starts_at->diffInSeconds($event->ends_at) : 0;
  19 | 
  20 |         foreach ($dates as $date) {
  21 |             if ($date->equalTo($event->starts_at)) continue;
  22 | 
  23 |             $exists = CalendarEvent::where('event_number', '!=', $event->event_number)
  24 |                 ->where('title', $event->title)
  25 |                 ->whereDate('starts_at', $date)
  26 |                 ->where('type', $event->type)
  27 |                 ->exists();
  28 | 
  29 |             if ($exists) continue;
  30 | 
  31 |             $cloned = $base->toArray();
  32 |             unset($cloned['event_number']);
  33 |             $cloned['starts_at'] = $date;
  34 |             $cloned['ends_at'] = $duration > 0 ? (clone $date)->addSeconds($duration) : null;
  35 | 
  36 |             CalendarEvent::create($cloned + [
  37 |                 'event_number' => app(CalendarNumberService::class)->generate(),
  38 |             ]);
  39 |         }
  40 |     }
  41 | 
  42 |     private function generateDates(CalendarEventRecurrence $recurrence, Carbon $start): array
  43 |     {
  44 |         $dates = [];
  45 |         $max = $recurrence->count ?? 52;
  46 |         $endDate = $recurrence->ends_at ? Carbon::parse($recurrence->ends_at) : null;
  47 |         $interval = max(1, $recurrence->interval);
  48 |         $current = clone $start;
  49 | 
  50 |         for ($i = 0; $i < $max; $i++) {
  51 |             if ($endDate && $current->greaterThan($endDate)) break;
  52 | 
  53 |             $dates[] = clone $current;
  54 | 
  55 |             match ($recurrence->frequency) {
  56 |                 'daily' => $current->addDays($interval),
  57 |                 'weekly' => $current->addWeeks($interval),
  58 |                 'monthly' => $current->addMonths($interval),
  59 |                 'yearly' => $current->addYears($interval),
  60 |                 default => $current->addWeeks($interval),
  61 |             };
  62 |         }
  63 | 
  64 |         return $dates;
  65 |     }
  66 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarReminderService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEventReminder;
   6 | use App\Models\User;
   7 | 
   8 | class CalendarReminderService
   9 | {
  10 |     public function __construct(
  11 |         protected CalendarNotificationService $notificationService,
  12 |         protected CalendarActivityService $activityService,
  13 |     ) {}
  14 | 
  15 |     public function snooze(CalendarEventReminder $reminder, ?int $minutes = 5): CalendarEventReminder
  16 |     {
  17 |         $reminder->update([
  18 |             'status' => 'snoozed',
  19 |             'snoozed_until' => now()->addMinutes($minutes),
  20 |         ]);
  21 | 
  22 |         return $reminder->fresh();
  23 |     }
  24 | 
  25 |     public function dismiss(CalendarEventReminder $reminder): CalendarEventReminder
  26 |     {
  27 |         $reminder->update([
  28 |             'status' => 'dismissed',
  29 |             'dismissed_at' => now(),
  30 |         ]);
  31 | 
  32 |         return $reminder->fresh();
  33 |     }
  34 | 
  35 |     public function send(CalendarEventReminder $reminder): void
  36 |     {
  37 |         if ($reminder->status === 'sent' || $reminder->status === 'dismissed') return;
  38 | 
  39 |         $event = $reminder->event;
  40 |         $user = $reminder->user;
  41 | 
  42 |         if ($user) {
  43 |             $this->notificationService->notifyReminderDue($event, $user);
  44 |         }
  45 | 
  46 |         $reminder->update([
  47 |             'status' => 'sent',
  48 |             'sent_at' => now(),
  49 |         ]);
  50 |     }
  51 | 
  52 |     public function processDue(): int
  53 |     {
  54 |         $sent = 0;
  55 | 
  56 |         CalendarEventReminder::where('status', 'pending')
  57 |             ->where(function ($q) {
  58 |                 $q->whereNotNull('remind_at')->where('remind_at', '<=', now())
  59 |                   ->orWhere(function ($q2) {
  60 |                       $q2->whereNull('remind_at')
  61 |                          ->whereNotNull('offset_minutes')
  62 |                          ->whereHas('event', fn ($e) => $e->where('starts_at', '<=', now()->addMinutes(
  63 |                              (new CalendarEventReminder)->rawOffset()
  64 |                          )));
  65 |                   });
  66 |             })
  67 |             ->chunk(50, function ($reminders) use (&$sent) {
  68 |                 foreach ($reminders as $reminder) {
  69 |                     $this->send($reminder);
  70 |                     $sent++;
  71 |                 }
  72 |             });
  73 | 
  74 |         CalendarEventReminder::where('status', 'snoozed')
  75 |             ->where('snoozed_until', '<=', now())
  76 |             ->chunk(50, function ($reminders) use (&$sent) {
  77 |                 foreach ($reminders as $reminder) {
  78 |                     $this->send($reminder);
  79 |                     $sent++;
  80 |                 }
  81 |             });
  82 | 
  83 |         return $sent;
  84 |     }
  85 | }

===== app\Services\Calendar\CalendarTaskSyncService.php =====

   1 | <?php
   2 | 
   3 | namespace App\Services\Calendar;
   4 | 
   5 | use App\Models\CalendarEvent;
   6 | use App\Models\Task;
   7 | use App\Models\User;
   8 | use App\Services\Task\TaskNumberService;
   9 | 
  10 | class CalendarTaskSyncService
  11 | {
  12 |     public function __construct(
  13 |         protected TaskNumberService $taskNumberService,
  14 |         protected CalendarActivityService $activityService,
  15 |     ) {}
  16 | 
  17 |     public function createTaskFromEvent(CalendarEvent $event, User $user): Task
  18 |     {
  19 |         $task = Task::create([
  20 |             'task_number' => $this->taskNumberService->generate(),
  21 |             'title' => $event->title,
  22 |             'description' => $event->description,
  23 |             'type' => 'calendar',
  24 |             'category' => $this->mapTypeToCategory($event->type),
  25 |             'status' => 'not_started',
  26 |             'priority' => $event->priority,
  27 |             'start_date' => $event->starts_at?->toDateString(),
  28 |             'due_date' => $event->ends_at?->toDateString() ?? $event->starts_at?->toDateString(),
  29 |             'created_by' => $user->id,
  30 |             'dossier_id' => $event->dossier_id,
  31 |             'client_id' => $event->client_id,
  32 |             'dossier_document_id' => $event->dossier_document_id,
  33 |             'finance_document_id' => $event->finance_document_id,
  34 |             'contract_id' => $event->contract_id,
  35 |             'authorization_id' => $event->authorization_id,
  36 |             'archive_record_id' => $event->archive_record_id,
  37 |         ]);
  38 | 
  39 |         $participantIds = $event->participants()
  40 |             ->whereIn('role', ['owner', 'assignee'])
  41 |             ->pluck('user_id')
  42 |             ->all();
  43 | 
  44 |         if ($participantIds) {
  45 |             $task->assignees()->sync($participantIds);
  46 |         }
  47 | 
  48 |         $event->update(['task_id' => $task->id]);
  49 | 
  50 |         $this->activityService->log($event, $user->id, 'task_created', null, ['task_id' => $task->id, 'task_number' => $task->task_number]);
  51 | 
  52 |         return $task;
  53 |     }
  54 | 
  55 |     public function syncEventDatesToTask(CalendarEvent $event): void
  56 |     {
  57 |         if (!$event->task_id) return;
  58 | 
  59 |         Task::where('id', $event->task_id)->update([
  60 |             'start_date' => $event->starts_at?->toDateString(),
  61 |             'due_date' => $event->ends_at?->toDateString() ?? $event->starts_at?->toDateString(),
  62 |         ]);
  63 |     }
  64 | 
  65 |     public function syncTaskStatusToEvent(Task $task): void
  66 |     {
  67 |         CalendarEvent::where('task_id', $task->id)->each(function (CalendarEvent $event) use ($task) {
  68 |             $event->update([
  69 |                 'status' => $task->status === 'completed' ? 'completed'
  70 |                     : ($task->status === 'cancelled' ? 'cancelled'
  71 |                         : ($task->status === 'in_progress' ? 'in_progress' : 'scheduled')),
  72 |             ]);
  73 |         });
  74 |     }
  75 | 
  76 |     private function mapTypeToCategory(string $type): string
  77 |     {
  78 |         return match ($type) {
  79 |             'client_follow_up' => 'client_follow_up',
  80 |             'finance_follow_up' => 'finance',
  81 |             'authorization_follow_up' => 'authorization',
  82 |             'contract_follow_up' => 'contract',
  83 |             'archive_follow_up' => 'archive',
  84 |             'meeting', 'deadline', 'task' => 'general_admin',
  85 |             default => 'general_admin',
  86 |         };
  87 |     }
  88 | }

===== resources/js/components/layout/navigation.ts =====

   1 | import { BadgeDollarSign } from 'lucide-react';
   2 | import { appRoutes } from '@/lib/appRoutes';
   3 | import type { AppRoute, AppRouteKey } from '@/lib/appRoutes';
   4 | 
   5 | export type NavigationItem = {
   6 |     key: string;
   7 |     labelKey: string;
   8 |     href?: string;
   9 |     icon?: AppRoute['icon'];
  10 |     enabled?: boolean;
  11 |     badgeKey?: string;
  12 |     children?: NavigationItem[];
  13 | };
  14 | 
  15 | export type NavigationGroup = {
  16 |     labelKey: string;
  17 |     items: NavigationItem[];
  18 | };
  19 | 
  20 | function routeByKey(key: AppRouteKey): AppRoute {
  21 |     const route = appRoutes.find((item) => item.key === key);
  22 | 
  23 |     if (!route) {
  24 |         throw new Error(`Missing app route: ${key}`);
  25 |     }
  26 | 
  27 |     return route;
  28 | }
  29 | 
  30 | const financeParent: NavigationItem = {
  31 |     key: 'financeParent',
  32 |     labelKey: 'nav.finance',
  33 |     href: '/finance',
  34 |     icon: BadgeDollarSign,
  35 |     enabled: true,
  36 |     children: [
  37 |         { ...routeByKey('finance'), labelKey: 'nav.financeOverview' },
  38 |         routeByKey('financeDocuments'),
  39 |         routeByKey('financePayments'),
  40 |         routeByKey('financeMonthly'),
  41 |         routeByKey('financeTemplates'),
  42 |         routeByKey('financeSettings'),
  43 |     ],
  44 | };
  45 | 
  46 | export const navigationGroups: NavigationGroup[] = [
  47 |     {
  48 |         labelKey: 'nav.groups.principal',
  49 |         items: appRoutes
  50 |             .filter((route) => route.group === 'principal')
  51 |             .map((route) => ({ ...route })),
  52 |     },
  53 |     {
  54 |         labelKey: 'nav.groups.followUp',
  55 |         items: appRoutes
  56 |             .filter((route) => route.group === 'followUp')
  57 |             .map((route) => ({ ...route })),
  58 |     },
  59 |     {
  60 |         labelKey: 'nav.groups.management',
  61 |         items: [
  62 |             financeParent,
  63 |             ...appRoutes
  64 |                 .filter((route) => route.group === 'management' && route.key === 'archives')
  65 |                 .map((route) => ({ ...route })),
  66 |         ],
  67 |     },
  68 |     {
  69 |         labelKey: 'nav.groups.administration',
  70 |         items: appRoutes
  71 |             .filter((route) => route.group === 'administration')
  72 |             .map((route) => ({ ...route })),
  73 |     },
  74 | ];

 284 | 
 285 | export function isValidHref(href: unknown): href is string {
 286 |     return typeof href === 'string' && href.trim().length > 0 && href !== '#';
 287 | }
 288 | 
 289 | function splitRoutePath(path: string) {
 290 |     const [pathname, query = ''] = path.split('?');
 291 | 
 292 |     return {
 293 |         pathname,
 294 |         query,
 295 |         params: new URLSearchParams(query),
 296 |     };
 297 | }
 298 | 
 299 | export function isActivePath(currentPath: string, itemPath: string) {
 300 |     if (!isValidHref(itemPath)) {
 301 |         return false;
 302 |     }
 303 | 
 304 |     const current = splitRoutePath(currentPath);
 305 |     const item = splitRoutePath(itemPath);
 306 | 
 307 |     if (item.pathname === '/') {
 308 |         return current.pathname === '/';
 309 |     }
 310 | 
 311 |     if (item.query) {
 312 |         if (current.pathname !== item.pathname) {
 313 |             return false;
 314 |         }
 315 | 
 316 |         return Array.from(item.params.entries()).every(
 317 |             ([key, value]) => current.params.get(key) === value,
 318 |         );
 319 |     }
 320 | 
 321 |     if (item.pathname === '/finance') {
 322 |         return current.pathname === '/finance' && !current.query;
 323 |     }
 324 | 
 325 |     return current.pathname === item.pathname || current.pathname.startsWith(`${item.pathname}/`);
 326 | }

===== resources/js/components/layout/AppSidebar.tsx =====

   1 | import { router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { Building2, ChevronRight, PanelLeftClose, PanelLeftOpen } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { useState } from 'react';
   4 | import { toast } from 'sonner';
   5 | import { AppBadge } from '@/components/ui/AppBadge';
   6 | import { AppTooltip } from '@/components/ui/AppTooltip';
   7 | import { navigationGroups } from '@/components/layout/navigation';
   8 | import type { NavigationItem } from '@/components/layout/navigation';
   9 | import { isActivePath, isValidHref } from '@/lib/appRoutes';
  10 | import { useTranslation } from '@/lib/i18n';
  11 | import { useTheme } from '@/providers/ThemeProvider';
  12 | 
  13 | export function AppSidebar() {
  14 |     const { t } = useTranslation();
  15 |     const { sidebarCollapsed, toggleSidebar } = useTheme();
  16 |     const page = usePage();
  17 |     const currentPath = page.url;
  18 |     const [expandedParents, setExpandedParents] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set(['financeParent']));
  19 | 
  20 |     function goTo(href: string, enabled: boolean) {
  21 |         if (!enabled || !isValidHref(href)) {
  22 |             toast.info('This module is not ready yet.');
  23 |             return;
  24 |         }
  25 | 
  26 |         router.visit(href);
  27 |     }
  28 | 
  29 |     function toggleExpand(key: string) {
  30 |         setExpandedParents((prev) => {
  31 |             const next = new Set(prev);
  32 |             if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);
  33 |             else next.add(key);
  34 |             return next;
  35 |         });
  36 |     }
  37 | 
  38 |     function renderItem(item: NavigationItem, level = 0) {
  39 |         const hasChildren = Boolean(item.children?.length);
  40 |         const isExpanded = expandedParents.has(item.key);
  41 |         const Icon = item.icon;
  42 |         const label = t(item.labelKey);
  43 |         const itemEnabled = item.enabled ?? true;
  44 |         const isItemActive = Boolean(item.href && item.enabled && isActivePath(currentPath, item.href));
  45 |         const active = hasChildren ? false : isItemActive;
  46 | 
  47 |         const buttonContent = (
  48 |             <button
  49 |                 key={item.key}
  50 |                 type="button"
  51 |                 onClick={() => {
  52 |                     if (hasChildren) toggleExpand(item.key);
  53 |                     else if (item.href) goTo(item.href, itemEnabled);
  54 |                 }}
  55 |                 className={[
  56 |                     'crm-nav-item group w-full text-sm font-semibold',
  57 |                     active ? 'crm-nav-item-active' : '',
  58 |                     !active && itemEnabled ? '' : '',
  59 |                     !itemEnabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-45' : '',
  60 |                     sidebarCollapsed ? 'mx-auto h-10 w-10 justify-center px-0' : 'justify-start',
  61 |                     level > 0 && !sidebarCollapsed ? 'text-[13px]' : '',
  62 |                 ].join(' ')}
  63 |                 style={{ paddingLeft: !sidebarCollapsed ? `${12 + level * 14}px` : undefined }}
  64 |             >
  65 |                 {Icon ? <Icon size={level > 0 ? 16 : 18} className="shrink-0 text-current" /> : null}
  66 | 
  67 |                 {!sidebarCollapsed ? (
  68 |                     <>
  69 |                         <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-left">{label}</span>
  70 |                         {item.badgeKey ? <AppBadge tone="neutral">{t(item.badgeKey)}</AppBadge> : null}
  71 |                         {hasChildren ? (
  72 |                             <ChevronRight
  73 |                                 size={15}
  74 |                                 className={`shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${isExpanded ? 'rotate-90' : ''}`}
  75 |                             />
  76 |                         ) : null}
  77 |                     </>
  78 |                 ) : null}
  79 |             </button>
  80 |         );
  81 | 
  82 |         return (
  83 |             <div key={item.key}>
  84 |                 {sidebarCollapsed && item.icon ? (
  85 |                     <AppTooltip label={label} position="right">
  86 |                         {buttonContent}
  87 |                     </AppTooltip>
  88 |                 ) : buttonContent}
  89 | 
  90 |                 {hasChildren && !sidebarCollapsed ? (
  91 |                     <div className={[
  92 |                         'ml-2 mt-1 space-y-1 overflow-hidden border-l border-[var(--crm-border)] pl-2 transition-all duration-300',
  93 |                         isExpanded ? 'max-h-[360px] opacity-100' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0',
  94 |                     ].join(' ')}>
  95 |                         {item.children!.map((child) => renderItem(child, level + 1))}
  96 |                     </div>
  97 |                 ) : null}
  98 |             </div>
  99 |         );
 100 |     }
 101 | 
 102 |     return (
 103 |         <aside className={[
 104 |             'crm-sidebar hidden h-screen shrink-0 overflow-hidden lg:sticky lg:top-0 lg:flex lg:flex-col transition-all duration-300',
 105 |             sidebarCollapsed ? 'w-[72px]' : 'w-[264px]',
 106 |         ].join(' ')}>
 107 |             <div className={[
 108 |                 'flex h-[72px] items-center border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] transition-all duration-300',
 109 |                 sidebarCollapsed ? 'justify-center px-0' : 'justify-between px-5',
 110 |             ].join(' ')}>
 111 |                 <button
 112 |                     type="button"
 113 |                     onClick={() => goTo('/', true)}
 114 |                     className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 text-left"
 115 |                 >
 116 |                     <div className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[var(--crm-radius-md)] bg-[var(--crm-gold)] text-black shadow-[var(--crm-shadow-gold)]">
 117 |                         <Building2 size={19} />
 118 |                     </div>
 119 | 
 120 |                     {!sidebarCollapsed ? (
 121 |                         <div className="min-w-0">
 122 |                             <p className="truncate text-sm font-black tracking-[-0.02em] text-[var(--crm-text)]">ARCHI LBO <span className="text-[var(--crm-gold)]">OS</span></p>
 123 |                             <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">{t('app.description')}</p>
 124 |                         </div>
 125 |                     ) : null}
 126 |                 </button>
 127 | 
 128 |                 {!sidebarCollapsed ? (
 129 |                     <button
 130 |                         type="button"
 131 |                         onClick={toggleSidebar}
 132 |                         className="crm-action-button h-8 w-8 px-0 text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]"
 133 |                         title="Collapse sidebar"
 134 |                     >
 135 |                         <PanelLeftClose size={15} />
 136 |                     </button>
 137 |                 ) : null}
 138 |             </div>
 139 | 
 140 |             {sidebarCollapsed ? (
 141 |                 <div className="flex justify-center border-b border-[var(--crm-border)] py-3">
 142 |                     <button
 143 |                         type="button"
 144 |                         onClick={toggleSidebar}
 145 |                         className="crm-action-button h-9 w-9 px-0 text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]"
 146 |                         title="Expand sidebar"
 147 |                     >
 148 |                         <PanelLeftOpen size={15} />
 149 |                     </button>
 150 |                 </div>
 151 |             ) : null}
 152 | 
 153 |             <nav className="crm-scroll-thin flex-1 space-y-6 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4">
 154 |                 {navigationGroups.map((group) => (
 155 |                     <section key={group.labelKey}>
 156 |                         {!sidebarCollapsed ? (
 157 |                             <p className="mb-2 px-3 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[var(--crm-text-soft)]">
 158 |                                 {t(group.labelKey)}
 159 |                             </p>
 160 |                         ) : null}
 161 | 
 162 |                         <div className="space-y-1">
 163 |                             {group.items.map((item) => renderItem(item))}
 164 |                         </div>
 165 |                     </section>
 166 |                 ))}
 167 |             </nav>
 168 |         </aside>
 169 |     );
 170 | }

===== resources/js/components/layout/AppTopbar.tsx =====

   1 | import { router, usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';
   2 | import { Bell, CalendarDays, LogOut, MessageSquare, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
   3 | import { AppGlobalSearch } from '@/components/layout/AppGlobalSearch';
   4 | import { ThemeToggle } from '@/components/layout/ThemeToggle';
   5 | 
   6 | function UnreadBadge({ count }: { count: number }) {
   7 |     if (count <= 0) return null;
   8 |     return (
   9 |         <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex min-w-[16px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500 px-1 text-[9px] font-bold leading-none text-white">
  10 |             {count > 99 ? '99+' : count}
  11 |         </span>
  12 |     );
  13 | }
  14 | 
  15 | export function AppTopbar() {
  16 |     const { auth } = usePage().props as { auth: { user?: { unread_notifications?: number; unread_messages?: number } } };
  17 |     const unreadNotifs = auth?.user?.unread_notifications ?? 0;
  18 |     const unreadChats = auth?.user?.unread_messages ?? 0;
  19 | 
  20 |     function handleLogout() {
  21 |         router.post('/logout');
  22 |     }
  23 | 
  24 |     return (
  25 |         <header className="crm-topbar sticky top-0 z-40">
  26 |             <div className="flex h-full items-center gap-3 px-4 lg:px-6">
  27 |                 <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  28 |                     <div className="max-w-[620px]">
  29 |                         <AppGlobalSearch />
  30 |                     </div>
  31 |                 </div>
  32 | 
  33 |                 <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
  34 |                     <button
  35 |                         type="button"
  36 |                         onClick={() => router.visit('/dossiers')}
  37 |                         className="crm-action-button-primary crm-action-button hidden sm:inline-flex"
  38 |                     >
  39 |                         <Plus size={15} />
  40 |                         New
  41 |                     </button>
  42 | 
  43 |                     <button
  44 |                         type="button"
  45 |                         onClick={() => router.visit('/finance/documents?tab=monthly')}
  46 |                         className="crm-action-button hidden md:inline-flex"
  47 |                         title="Monthly summary"
  48 |                     >
  49 |                         <CalendarDays size={15} />
  50 |                     </button>
  51 | 
  52 |                     <button
  53 |                         type="button"
  54 |                         onClick={() => router.visit('/inbox')}
  55 |                         className="crm-action-button relative hidden h-9 w-9 px-0 sm:inline-flex"
  56 |                         title="Messages"
  57 |                     >
  58 |                         <MessageSquare size={15} />
  59 |                         <UnreadBadge count={unreadChats} />
  60 |                     </button>
  61 | 
  62 |                     <button
  63 |                         type="button"
  64 |                         onClick={() => router.visit('/notifications')}
  65 |                         className="crm-action-button relative h-9 w-9 px-0 sm:inline-flex"
  66 |                         title="Notifications"
  67 |                     >
  68 |                         <Bell size={15} />
  69 |                         <UnreadBadge count={unreadNotifs} />
  70 |                     </button>
  71 | 
  72 |                     <ThemeToggle />
  73 | 
  74 |                     <button
  75 |                         type="button"
  76 |                         onClick={handleLogout}
  77 |                         className="crm-action-button h-9 w-9 px-0"
  78 |                         aria-label="Logout"
  79 |                         title="Logout"
  80 |                     >
  81 |                         <LogOut size={15} />
  82 |                     </button>
  83 |                 </div>
  84 |             </div>
  85 |         </header>
  86 |     );
  87 | }

===== resources/js/components/layout/AppShell.tsx =====

   1 | import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
   2 | import { AppMobileNav } from '@/components/layout/AppMobileNav';
   3 | import { AppSidebar } from '@/components/layout/AppSidebar';
   4 | import { AppTopbar } from '@/components/layout/AppTopbar';
   5 | import { useTranslation } from '@/lib/i18n';
   6 | 
   7 | type AppShellProps = {
   8 |     eyebrowKey?: string;
   9 |     titleKey: string;
  10 |     subtitleKey?: string;
  11 |     action?: ReactNode;
  12 |     children: ReactNode;
  13 | };
  14 | 
  15 | export function AppShell({ eyebrowKey, titleKey, subtitleKey, action, children }: AppShellProps) {
  16 |     const { t } = useTranslation();
  17 | 
  18 |     return (
  19 |         <div className="crm-shell min-h-screen text-[var(--crm-text)]">
  20 |             <div className="flex min-h-screen">
  21 |                 <AppSidebar />
  22 | 
  23 |                 <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
  24 |                     <AppTopbar />
  25 | 
  26 |                     <main className="crm-page pb-28 lg:pb-[var(--crm-page-pad)]">
  27 |                         <header className="crm-panel-flat px-5 py-4">
  28 |                             <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
  29 |                                 <div className="min-w-0">
  30 |                                     {eyebrowKey ? <p className="crm-eyebrow">{t(eyebrowKey)}</p> : null}
  31 |                                     <h1 className="mt-2 text-[24px] font-bold leading-none tracking-[-0.02em] text-[var(--crm-text)]">
  32 |                                         {t(titleKey)}
  33 |                                     </h1>
  34 |                                     {subtitleKey ? (
  35 |                                         <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--crm-text-muted)]">
  36 |                                             {t(subtitleKey)}
  37 |                                         </p>
  38 |                                     ) : null}
  39 |                                 </div>
  40 | 
  41 |                                 {action ? (
  42 |                                     <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  43 |                                         {action}
  44 |                                     </div>
  45 |                                 ) : null}
  46 |                             </div>
  47 |                         </header>
  48 | 
  49 |                         {children}
  50 |                     </main>
  51 |                 </div>
  52 |             </div>
  53 | 
  54 |             <AppMobileNav />
  55 |         </div>
  56 |     );
  57 | }
